Across five repeated closed-loop trials on a mixed-terrain route totalling around $6.4$ km, the proposed system increases overall autonomy from $59.6\%$ to $97.0\%$, reduces human interventions from $11$ to $3$, and increases the mean distance between interventions (MDBI) from $69.2$ m to $412.9$ m compared with geometry-only navigation. These results demonstrate that physically grounded VLM-based terrain assessment can substantially extend autonomous navigation beyond the limitations of geometry alone, while preserving efficient geometric planning as the nominal mode.

</details>


### [145] [Do Spinning Radar Doppler Velocity Measurements Improve Vehicle Detection and Tracking?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21000)
*Eric Xie,Daniil Lisus,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于自旋FMCW雷达的多普勒速度测量对自动驾驶车辆检测与跟踪性能的提升效果，并提出了一套自动化标注流程生成了大规模地面实测数据，用于训练与评估检测/跟踪网络，实验显示多普勒信息能有效提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋FMCW雷达因其全天候、360°视野优势，逐渐应用于自动驾驶感知系统。近期研究发现自旋雷达能够提供每一角度的多普勒速度信息，但是否能直接提升目标检测与跟踪效果尚未得到量化验证。此外，缺乏可用含多普勒信息与精确标签的大规模数据集也是限制因素。

Method: 1. 对检测任务：利用估算的自车运动消除雷达图像多普勒失真后输入检测网络。2. 对跟踪任务：提出新方法，通过每辆车的多普勒速度估算为跟踪器提供运动先验。3. 数据集方面：构建自动化标签流程，结合多种微调后的现成激光雷达检测器，对Boreas Road Trip数据集（643km）完成车辆目标自动标注，并将其投映到雷达，实现数据集扩展。

Result: 实验表明，多普勒失真校正操作可使目标检测平均精度提升最多2.37个百分点。对多目标跟踪来说，采用多普勒速度作为先验可使跟踪准确率（MOTA）较零速度初始提升13.68个百分点，并取得了接近真实先验时（99.7%）的效果。

Conclusion: 基于自旋FMCW雷达的多普勒速度信息，能有效提升自动驾驶车辆目标检测与跟踪的鲁棒性和准确性；同时，自动化标签流程极大丰富了相关数据集，为后续研究和工业应用提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Spinning frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radars have been gaining popularity in autonomous vehicle perception on account of their robustness to adverse weather conditions and 360° field of view. Recently, scanning radars have also been shown capable of generating per-azimuth Doppler velocity. In this paper, we investigate whether these Doppler velocity measurements improve spinning radar vehicle detection and tracking performance. For detection, we estimate the ego motion and use it to undo the Doppler range distortion of the radar image before passing it to a network. For tracking, we propose a new way to estimate a per-vehicle velocity and use it as a prior for the tracker's motion model. Since Doppler-enabled spinning radar data is not available in any dataset with ground-truth dynamic object labels, our first contribution is an automatic labelling pipeline that uses an ensemble of fine-tuned off-the-shelf lidar detectors to label all 643 km of the Boreas Road Trip dataset. We then transfer detections to radar, and use over 250 km of vehicle-dense sequences as ground-truth training data. By training and evaluating two state-of-the-art detectors, we show that Doppler undistortion can improve detection accuracy by up to $2.37$ points on mean average precision. Furthermore, we show that the Doppler velocity prior can improve tracking accuracy by $13.68$ points on multi-object tracking accuracy (MOTA) versus the zero-velocity initialization baseline, while achieving $99.7\%$ of the MOTA obtained using ground-truth velocities as the prior.

</details>


### [146] [Project SCOUT: Interceptor Drone for Perimeter Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21005)
*Azmain Yousuf,Siwei Cai,Knut Peterson,Lifeng Zhou,David Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCOUT是一个集成视觉检测和控制的无人机对抗系统，能够实时、无需外部基础设施下对入侵无人机进行高效拦截和跟踪，在实际测试中表现出92.2%的有效检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机非法入侵频发，传统反制系统依赖外部设备，易受干扰且布局受限。因此，需要一种抗干扰、能独立运行的无人机反制方法，用于敏感区域的周界防御。

Method: 提出了SCOUT框架，将TensorRT加速的目标检测与ByteTrack目标关联和轻量级状态机结合，在防御无人机自身解析和生成控制策略，无需地面站或外部传感器，直接实现目标识别、轨迹过滤和实时运动调整。

Result: 在“守门”式实地测试中，SCOUT在实时帧率下对目标保持92.2%的有效检测，并能自主调整位置屏障入侵无人机，硬件集成和飞行实验证明了其实用性。

Conclusion: SCOUT验证了无需外部硬件即可实施高效空中视觉跟踪与闭环控制，展现了面向周界防御的无人机反制系统的可行性。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of unauthorized unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) has created a growing need for robust, jamming-resistant counter-UAV systems for perimeter defense. This paper presents \textbf{SCOUT} (Spatial Computation for Optimized UAV Tracking), a ROS-integrated onboard perception and control framework for real-time aerial defense against incoming UAVs. SCOUT performs visual detection, target association, track filtering, and control command generation directly onboard the defender UAV, without relying on external sensing infrastructure or ground-station computation. To provide stable control inputs, the perception pipeline combines TensorRT-accelerated drone detection with ByteTrack-based association and a lightweight track-retention state machine. The state machine rejects abrupt target jumps and maintains short-term target continuity during temporary detection degradation, reducing unstable control responses caused by false detections or target switching. We evaluate the proposed architecture through an integrated hardware deployment executing a planar ``goalkeeping'' interception strategy. In this setting, the defender UAV tracks the incoming target and adjusts its motion to maintain a blocking configuration near the protected boundary. Real-world flight results show that SCOUT maintains valid target detections for 92.2\% of frames while operating at real-time onboard detection rates, demonstrating the feasibility of visual tracking and closed-loop control for UAV perimeter defense. A video demonstration of the end-to-end perimeter defense operation is available online. https://figshare.com/s/497befc033091fc0b84f

</details>


### [147] [SPARROW: Survival-POMCP for Adaptive Robot Routing, Observation, and Waiting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21008)
*Hshmat Sahak,Aoran Jiao,Nicholas Rhinehart,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对临时障碍物导航问题的新规划器SPARROW，可动态决策等待、绕行或获取更多信息，提升了机器人导航效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对临时障碍物时，机器人常需在等待障碍清除、寻找新路线或获取更多障碍信息之间做出决策，传统方法效率和灵活性有限。

Method: 将有临时障碍的图导航建模为部分可观测半马尔可夫决策过程（POSMDP），引入基于部分可观测蒙特卡洛规划（POMCP）的SPARROW规划器，利用粒子滤波在障碍类别及清除时间上保持置信度，并在线学习障碍清除的生存模型，引入学习价值准则权衡数据采集与未来成本。

Result: 在两种仿真环境和多种障碍类别下，SPARROW相比于已有的OSCAR方法使平均达标时间减少12-26%；实际移动机器人测试中，SPARROW将平均达标时间减少20.5%。

Conclusion: SPARROW能够在环境动态变化时灵活地选择观察、等待或绕行策略，并显著提升机器人在有临时障碍环境下的导航效率，优于常规生存模型方法。

Abstract: Temporary obstacles that may block a robot's planned route create a sequential navigation problem: a robot must decide whether to wait for a blockage to clear, reroute, or acquire more information about the obstacle before acting. We formulate graph navigation among temporary obstacles as a partially observable semi-Markov decision process and introduce SPARROW, a belief-space planner built on Partially Observable Monte Carlo Planning (POMCP). SPARROW searches over traversal, observation, and finite-duration waiting actions while maintaining a particle belief over latent obstacle classes and clearance times. Class-conditioned survival models are learned online from both clearance observations and right-censored encounters where the robot reroutes before clearance is observed. A generative model simulates obstacle arrivals and clearances as each action unfolds, so the planner can account for blockages that may occur along alternative routes. We further introduce a value-of-learning criterion that trades the immediate cost of collecting labelled survival data against its expected reduction in future navigation regret. Across two simulation graphs and multiple obstacle-class settings, SPARROW reduces mean time-to-goal by 12-26% relative to OSCAR, a recent survival-based method for the same problem. On a physical mobile robot, SPARROW reduces mean time-to-goal by 20.5% relative to OSCAR while selectively observing, waiting, and rerouting as environment conditions change.

</details>


### [148] [Towards Effective Visual-Inertial SLAM with Passive-Only Sensors for Low-Cost Autonomous Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21015)
*Grant Schwidder,David Widhalm,Junaed Sattar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出并验证了在成本低于1万美元的水下机器人平台上，仅使用低价消费级传感器（例如立体相机、MEMS IMU和深度传感器），即可实现高质量的视觉-惯性同步定位与建图（VI-SLAM），降低了先进水下机器人导航技术应用门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高端水下机器人依赖昂贵的传感器（如DVL和USBL），使得相关自主导航技术难以普及到更广泛的应用和业余爱好者。本研究旨在让经济实惠的AUV也能实现高水平的自主定位与建图，从而推动该领域的大众化和普及。

Method: 采用开源、价位相近的AUV平台，搭载消费级立体摄像头、MEMS IMU和深度传感器，在6自由度真实水下环境中进行实验。对现有SLAM软件包性能进行评估，并提出了针对水下作业特殊挑战的传感器融合优化策略。

Result: 实验表明，在低成本配置下，通过适当的软件和传感器融合优化，可以实现稳健、可用性的VI-SLAM导航系统，满足复杂实时水下任务的精度要求。

Conclusion: 本研究证明，水下机器人无需依赖昂贵行业级硬件，即可实现有效的同步定位与建图，为低成本AUV在科研和业余领域的广泛应用树立了技术基准。

Abstract: Improvements to Visual-Inertial Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (VI-SLAM) for low-cost autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) are critical for transitioning advanced marine robotics from specialized labs to broader research and hobbyist applications. While high-end AUVs typically rely on expensive sensor suites - such as Doppler Velocity Logs (DVLs) and Ultra-Short Baseline (USBL) systems - this work demonstrates that robust, high-quality navigation is achievable using a sub-$10, 000(USD) platform equipped only with inexpensive consumer-grade sensors. By leveraging a similarly priced, open-source AUV, we evaluate the performance of stereo cameras, Micro-electromechanical System (MEMS)-based IMUs, and depth sensors in a fully unconstrained 6-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) underwater environment. We analyze the efficacy of off-the-shelf SLAM packages and propose optimizations for sensor fusion to mitigate the visual and physical challenges of untethered underwater operation. Our results prove that a usable SLAM solution can be accessible to the masses, providing a benchmark for expectations in demanding, real-time maritime missions without the financial barrier of industrial-grade hardware.

</details>


### [149] [Catch Me If You Can: Real-Time Feedback Denoising for Responsive VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21022)
*Yiheng Ji,Xingru Zhou,Luis Sentis,Mingyo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种VLA-Feedback架构，将低频扩散式规划与高频视觉反馈结合，提升机器人在动态环境中的操作反应与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA方法结合了视觉-语言模型与强大的动作生成策略，但动作生成通常为开放环执行，对环境动态变化（如物体移动、接触变化）反应迟缓，限制了在真实世界中的实用性。

Method: 提出VLA-Feedback双时间尺度结构。低频部分用扩散模型进行长期动作规划；高频部分在动作执行前，利用最新视觉观测，仅对扩散过程最后一步进行快速反馈修正，无需完整重跑扩散模型。

Result: VLA-Feedback在静态任务上与现有方法持平，在动态仿真任务上平均成功率从27.5%提升到85.0%，在真实机器人任务上从51%提升到73%。

Conclusion: 通过结合扩散规划的表达能力与轻量级实时反馈，VLA-Feedback显著提升了机器人在动态环境中的适应性与操作成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong generalization in robotic manipulation by combining semantic knowledge from pretrained vision-language models with expressive action-generation policies. Diffusion-based action generators are particularly effective for modeling temporally coherent action chunks, but these chunks are typically executed open-loop after inference. This limits responsiveness when objects move, contacts change, or the scene evolves during execution. We propose VLA-Feedback, a two-timescale architecture that combines low-frequency diffusion planning with high-frequency visual feedback. Rather than fully denoising an action chunk before execution, VLA-Feedback retains its final denoising step as a lightweight feedback interface, allowing each action to be corrected using the latest observation before it is executed. This design preserves the expressiveness of the diffusion planner while enabling real-time action correction without rerunning the full vision-language diffusion model. VLA-Feedback matched GR00T on static LIBERO tasks while improving average success on dynamic simulation tasks from 27.5% to 85.0%. On real-robot tasks, it improved average success from 51% to 73%. Additional materials can be found on our project page: https://vla-feedback.github.io.

</details>


### [150] [Adapting Rigid-Body Dynamics Derivatives for Constraint Embedding Closed-Chain Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21024)
*Daniel J. Volpi,Patrick M. Wensing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了一阶导数刚体动力学算法，使其适用于通过约束嵌入建模的闭链系统，并推导了可处理更一般关节类型的动力学导数算法。结果表明该方法能够更为准确和有效地支持复杂的动力学计算，尤其适合于实时控制与可微分仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有的一阶导数动力学算法多适用于开链系统，对闭链机构（即存在约束的系统）支持有限。且现有效率高的导数算法往往假定关节速度对构型是局部不变的，这一假设并不总是成立。在机器人更加复杂和完整的模型、特别是考虑到闭链和完整驱动的场景下，亟需能够适用约束嵌入闭链动力学的推导方法。

Method: 作者移除了对关节速度影响构型局部不变的假设，针对通过约束嵌入的闭链模型，推导出了适用于更一般关节类型及其导数的新算法。同时，通过与传统的针状关节（pin-joint）模型及包含完整驱动、非局部闭环结构的更复杂模型进行对比，分析了新算法对计算复杂性的影响。

Result: 结果显示：当只模拟驱动运动学时，新增项对计算复杂性影响较小；但在驱动链中包含额外刚体（如电机转子）或考虑非局部环路时，计算成本增加。此外，算法能更准确地处理约束嵌入的驱动子机构动力学。

Conclusion: 提出的新算法能够扩展动力学导数计算到更广泛的模型类别，尤其是涉及约束闭链机构的机器人，提升了动力学计算的准确性和适用范围。该方法适合应用在模型预测控制(MPC)和可微分仿真等高精度动力学需求场景。

Abstract: This paper extends an existing algorithm for the first-order derivatives of rigid-body dynamics to the case of closed-chain kinematic systems modeled using constraint embed- ding. Many standard dynamics algorithms apply to both open- chain and constraint-embedded models, but existing efficient derivative methods assume joint velocity effects are locally config- uration invariant. We remove this assumption and derive adapted algorithms that extend dynamics derivatives to more general joint types, including those arising in constraint-embedded closed- chain models. Our results compare conventional pin-joint robot models with more complete actuation models that capture local closed chains. We show that the additional terms introduced by these generalizations have low computational impact when mod- eling actuation kinematics alone, but can incur higher cost when additional rigid bodies, such as motor rotors, are included in the actuation chain, or when considering non-local loops. Overall, these results enable more accurate dynamics computations for constraint-embedded actuation submechanisms to be adopted in model-predictive control and differentiable simulation.

</details>


### [151] [MAPLE-RF: Efficient Probabilistic RF Source Localization in Partially Explored Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21026)
*Haozhe Lei,Sundeep Rangan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在仅有部分已知环境地图的情况下（如机器人使用SLAM探索时），如何利用单次观测的信号定位无线电发射机，并比较了数字孪生射线追踪扩展法与基于深度学习的MAPLE-RF方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人探索新环境或未知环境时，往往没有完整的环境模型，而无线电信号传播受障碍物影响很大，因此亟需能在部分地图下进行有效定位的方法。

Method: 提出两种后验概率推断方法：（1）扩展数字孪生射线追踪法——对所有可能位置进行射线追踪，未探索区域视为无障碍，并在不同已知程度的地图上训练；（2）MAPLE-RF——将信号角度、信噪比等特征映射为网格通道，与已知度、占用度、视线通道结合，输入U-Net一次性对全部候选位置打分，无需推理期间射线追踪。

Result: 在室内仿真环境下，两种方法都需在不完全地图上训练才能取得好效果。数字孪生法在大多数定位指标上最准确，MAPLE-RF成本极低且在某些指标上逼近前者，均优于高斯及混合高斯基线。MAPLE-RF用于引导探索时，对源位置概率聚集更优。

Conclusion: 两种方法都能在部分已知地图下实现比传统方法更优的单次无线电源定位，MAPLE-RF在计算效率和实用性方面有显著优势，适合嵌入式或实时机器人应用。代码和数据将公开，为相关研究和应用提供资源。

Abstract: Localizing a radio-frequency (RF) transmitter from received signals often requires a model of the environment to predict how obstacles block and reflect the signal. In many robotic applications, however, only a partial map is available, particularly when a robot localizes the source while exploring with simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM). We study single-snapshot transmitter localization on such partially explored maps and compare two approaches that output a posterior over transmitter locations. The first extends a digital-twin method, which ray-traces every candidate location, to partial maps by treating unexplored space as free and training on mixed map coverage. The second, MAPLE-RF, encodes estimated path angles of arrival and signal-to-noise ratios as grid channels aligned with map knownness, occupancy, and line-of-sight visibility, and a U-Net scores all candidate positions in one pass without simulating propagation at inference. Ray-tracing simulations of indoor environments indicate that training on mixed map coverage is essential for both approaches. The digital-twin approach is more accurate on most single-snapshot metrics, while MAPLE-RF comes close at a query cost that does not depend on the propagation model and is more than two orders of magnitude below a fresh full-grid query with general-purpose ray tracing. Both outperform Gaussian and Gaussian-mixture baselines, and on exploration routes guided by its own estimates, fused MAPLE-RF posteriors place more probability near the source than the compared methods. Code and data will be released.

</details>


### [152] [DEXTERA: From a Single Image to Deployable Dexterous Manipulation via Real-to-Sim-to-Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21045)
*Jin Wu,Lianjie Yuan,Zeyan Sun,Yuanyuan Lei,Disi A,Bicheng Han,Fangzhou Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DEXTERA提出了一套自动化从真实到仿真再到真实的灵巧操作机器人数据处理和策略学习框架，大幅度降低数据收集和仿真建模成本，实现从单张RGB图像高效生成可部署操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中收集灵巧操作机器人数据代价高昂，虽然高保真物理模拟器可以放大量数据，却因数字孪生搭建复杂及仿真-现实落差，导致难以直接部署仿真学到的策略。作者希望通过自动化流程，降低搭建数字孪生门槛并弥合仿真-现实鸿沟。

Method: 提出DEXTERA四阶段流程：1）通过单张RGB图像，将场景分解为高斯背景与可交互模型，并用视觉语言模型推断物理参数；2）实现全局对齐、物体标准化及机器人校准；3）构建可扩展的仿真任务原语，通过VR远程操作及面向对象的轨迹生成；4）多模态策略接口，支持模仿和强化学习。

Result: 在13组任务、2个平台和6种策略架构评测，DEXTERA在视觉保真和3D重建上优于生成式基线，跨域轨迹重放证明物理一致性，仿真训练的策略可直接零样本部署到实机，仿真-实机联合训练成功率由29.2%提升到61.9%。

Conclusion: DEXTERA显著简化了灵巧操作机器人仿真建模和策略部署流程，有效填补仿真与真实之间的鸿沟，提高了策略在实机上的落地效果。

Abstract: Collecting real-world robot data for dexterous manipulation is costly and time-consuming. While high-fidelity physics simulators enable scalable data synthesis and policy learning, constructing deployment-ready digital twins manually remains labor-intensive, and residual visual, geometric, and dynamics gaps hinder reliable sim-to-real transfer. We present DEXTERA, an automated real-to-sim-to-real framework that transforms a single RGB image into deployable policies for dexterous manipulation across four unified stages: (1) single-image scene factorization into a static Gaussian background and interactive rigid or articulated assets with VLM-inferred physical parameters; (2) metric scene global alignment, object canonicalization, and morphology-balanced robot calibration; (3) scalable simulator task primitive construction, VR teleoperation, and object-centric trajectory synthesis; and (4) a shared multimodal policy interface supporting both imitation learning and reinforcement learning. We evaluate DEXTERA across 13 task-embodiment pairs, 2 dexterous robot platforms, and 6 policy architectures. Experimental results demonstrate that DEXTERA achieves superior visual fidelity and 3D geometric reconstruction compared to generative baselines, while cross-domain trajectory replays validate strong physical interaction consistency. Furthermore, simulation-only trained policies enable viable zero-shot real-robot deployment, while simulation-real co-training substantially improves mean physical policy success from 29.2% to 61.9% across diverse policy architectures.

</details>


### [153] [Constraint-Unified MPC for Over-Actuated Surface Vehicles with Post-Detection Fault Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21046)
*Sebastian Burmester,Ruiheng Jiang,Noa Sendlhofer,Raffaello D'Andrea,Aswin Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种集成方法，利用单个二次规划（QP），同时处理水面机器人编队演出时的轨迹跟踪、推力分配及单推力器的力和速率约束，还可根据推力器失效进行容错重构。该系统已真实部署并实地测试，展现出较高的精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对水上自主机器人编队表演，需要高精度地跟踪轨迹，同时受到单推力器的力和速率约束。在演出或实际应用中，推进器可能失效，因此需要一个能鲁棒应对故障、实时分配推力并保障性能的闭环控制系统。

Method: 作者设计并实现了一个统一的二次规划（QP）框架，将轨迹跟踪、推力分配、力和速率约束通过单一优化过程解决。推力器故障通过外部检测器输入的二元标志参与重构。系统与传统级联系统做了对比评估。

Result: 在真实湖面和展览中，系统表现出1.6cm均方根静止误差和4.3cm高速运动误差。单个推进器失效时最大误差约11.6cm，两台失效时误差约1.25m。与级联基线相比，统一方法在正常情况下精度相当但力消耗更低，在失效后鲁棒性显著更强，能始终贴近参考路径。

Conclusion: 统一二次规划方案可以更高效和健壮地解决多约束下的水面自主体轨迹跟踪和推力分配问题，对保证编队表演等应用的安全性和精度具有实际意义。

Abstract: Choreographed aquatic performances require small autonomous surface vehicles to track precise paths under per-thruster force and rate limits, including after thruster failures. We report a deployed system in which trajectory tracking, thrust allocation, and the per-thruster force and rate limits are resolved in a single quadratic program over the per-thruster commands, with fault reconfiguration entering through one binary flag per thruster from an external detector. The system has driven a fleet in live performances on Lake Zürich and at the Time Space Existence 2025 exhibition in Venice. The field campaign measures 1.6 cm root mean square position error in a 10-minute hold and 4.3 cm over a 10 m square at 0.6 m/s. At 0.5 m/s, losing the front thruster increases the error to 11.6 cm, while losing the starboard-side thruster increases it to 11.5 cm. Losing two thrusters simultaneously leaves the craft tracking a 0.4 m/s square with a root mean square position error of 1.25 m. Compared to our own cascaded baseline, the unified formulation tracks the nominal square to the same few centimeters and holds station more tightly with roughly half the thruster force. The architectures separate after a thruster failure, where the unified controller stays within 24 cm of the reference path while the cascade leaves it.

</details>


### [154] [PlantShade: Predicting Plant Shadows for Lighting-Aware Robotic Agricultural Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21059)
*Longchao Da,Xiaoou Liu,Xingjian Li,Lirong Xiang,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过引入植物生长与遮荫大数据集以及扩散模型生成方法，推进了植物遮荫模拟研究，并为农业和机器人领域提供基础支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物生长和农业生产对可持续发展至关重要，植被遮荫直接影响植物生长。现有对遮荫模拟的研究和数据资源有限，且植物遮荫具有复杂性和多样性，亟需科学、自动化的模拟方法支持现代农业管理。

Method: 1）构建包含大豆、西红柿、甜菜和草莓四种植物的遮荫数据集，数据涵盖多视角、多光照轨迹和不同生长阶段；2）提出基于扩散模型的生成式遮荫模拟方法，可对未知植物进行真实感强的遮荫生成，并引入时序条件实现遮荫的动态模拟。

Result: 模型能够灵活生成不同阶段、不同植物类型的真实感遮荫效果，通过定量和定性评估验证了其性能优越，可有效支持感知、照明、视角规划等农业与机器人应用场景。

Conclusion: 本工作为植物感知遮荫建模奠定基础，有助于提升智能农业及相关机器人技术，为作物管理和农业自动化带来实际意义和广泛应用前景。

Abstract: Plant growth and agricultural production form the foundation of a country's sustainable development and directly impact human livelihoods. Recent advances in frontier artificial intelligence have enabled scientific agriculture with strong potential to improve crop productivity. In this paper, we identify the importance and inherent complexity of plant shade simulation, as shading is a critical factor influencing plant growth. To advance this field and promote broader societal benefits, we focus on two main contributions. First, we introduce a comprehensive plant growth and shade dataset covering four plant species, including soybean, tomato, sugarbeet, and strawberry. The dataset includes top-down viewpoints with a supplementary light along a circular trajectory, casting dynamic shadows across multiple growth stages and diverse observation complexities. Second, we propose generative shade simulation based on diffusion models, enabling realistic shade generation for unseen plants and supporting downstream robotic tasks such as perception, lighting control, and view planning. The model incorporates temporal conditioning to facilitate flexible shade simulation across different time stages. We conduct both quantitative and qualitative evaluations to assess model performance. This work provides a foundational study for plant-aware shade modeling and has meaningful implications for broader agricultural and robotic applications.

</details>


### [155] [Design of Adaptive PID Controller Based On Asynchronous Advantage Actor Critic Learning Method for QuadCopter Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21082)
*Ali Jokar,Aria Alasty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将A3C算法与PID控制器结合，用于四旋翼的姿态与轨迹跟踪控制，相较A2C表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼无人机具有高度非线性和受扰动敏感的特点，传统PID控制器难以胜任复杂的控制需求。为此，亟需更智能、更动态的控制方法以提升控制性能。

Method: 提出一种控制系统，将异步优势行动者-评论家（A3C）算法与PID控制器结合，通过并行智能体与神经网络动态优化PID参数。并设计了系统辨识单元用于预测系统状态，实现最优控制策略。

Result: 仿真结果显示，A3C与A2C方法都能实现准确跟踪，但A3C算法在损失函数收敛方面表现更好，奖励数值和损失曲线都优于A2C，说明参数优化更为高效。

Conclusion: A3C与PID的结合有效提升了四旋翼控制性能，增强了智能控制与传统控制的适应性，实现了更高的控制精度和更优的参数优化能力。

Abstract: Quadcopters offer great utility in many applications, but their nonlinear nature and disturbance sensitivity present great control challenges. Basic PID controllers are generally not sophisticated enough to cope with these complexities. This paper suggests a control system that integrates the Asynchronous Advantage Actor-Critic (A3C) algorithm with a PID controller for quadcopter attitude and trajectory tracking. The A3C controller uses parallel agents to optimize PID parameters dynamically using a neural network. A system identification module for the complementary system makes predictions about system states for optimal control policy. The proposed framework was compared with a standard actor-critic (A2C) model. Simulation results verify that they both track accurately. However, the A3C-based controller converges much more for the loss function, as evidenced by reward figures and loss curves, demonstrating better parameter optimization. This shows that A3C-based approach results in improved performance for the control of quadcopter, effectively integrating reinforcement learning and traditional control to achieve higher adaptability.

</details>


### [156] [Dynamic Modeling and LQR Control of a Single Coaxial Drone with 2DOF Thrust Vectoring Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21099)
*Ali Jokar,Amin Talaeizadeh,Aria Alasty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新型共轴无人机，采用摆锤主动控制推力方向，降低了机械复杂度，设计了LQR控制器，经高保真仿真验证，具有节能和高效性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共轴无人机由于结构限制，横滚与俯仰受控能力不足，虽然通过复杂机械如滚珠盘可突破此限制，但带来更多复杂性。本研究希望用更简易的机械方案提高控制能力，兼顾能效与体积。

Method: 作者提出结合双自由度摆锤机构进行主动推力矢量控制，建立了包括机体、舵机臂和动力组件等完整惯性贡献的拉格朗日系统动力学模型，并基于悬停平衡点线性化设计了LQR控制器。同时，采用EKF融合GPS、气压计和IMU，实现高精度状态估计，仿真中考虑执行器动态和传感器噪声。

Result: 仿真结果显示，该新型共轴无人机方案在考虑真实操作条件下控制性能可靠，节省能耗且响应迅速。

Conclusion: 本文所提基于摆锤主动推力矢量和LQR控制的新结构，在降低机械复杂度前提下，实现了共轴无人机的稳定高效控制，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Coaxial rotor drones have generated considerable interest because of energy efficiency and small size, but they are afflicted with inherent underactuation for roll and pitch control, although systems like swashplates have circumvented this limitation at the cost of greater mechanical complexity. This work presents a novel coaxial drone supplemented by a two-degreesof-freedom pendulum mechanism for active thrust vectoring that offers a less mechanically complicated alternative. We develop a comprehensive Lagrangian dynamic model that does not ignore the inertial contributions of all the components, including body, servo arms, and motor assembly. A Linear Quadratic Regulator(LQR) is designed based on the linearized dynamics around the hover equilibrium. High-fidelity simulations taking actuator dynamics and sensor noise into account validate the proposed architecture. An Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) blends GPS, barometer, and IMU estimates with high accuracy for state estimation. The findings verify the potential and reliability of this approach for power-saving, rapid coaxial UAVs.

</details>


### [157] [Dynamics-Induced Commitment in Learning-Based Robotic Penalty Kicks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21100)
*Ruize Geng,Hao E. Zhang,Yisen Li,Yikai Wang,H. Eric Tseng,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了机器人竞技中的策略学习与身体动态约束之间的耦合，提出了一种新的分析方法，对惩罚射门场景下的人形与四足机器人互动策略进行了深入研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人对抗游戏中的学习不仅受限于策略性信息，还受到机器人本身动作可实现性的约束。研究这种耦合关系有助于理解机器人如何在动态约束下优化竞技表现。

Method: 构建了一个分层的人形-四足机器人点球系统，人形机器人射门动作通过自采集动捕数据初始化，四足机器人守门动作用强化学习训练。引入了DIC-Map(body-grounded分析方法)，用以估算动作持续能力、确定决策丧失点，并简化博弈结构，推导出最优策略分布的界。进一步分析了响应值平衡时学习通道的变化。

Result: 通过实验发现，关键动作承诺点出现在接触前约0.29秒，仅改变球速可明显影响守门响应策略。更换估算器的守门策略可使扑救率从0.240提升到0.472，而等待提升的识别准确率仅能提升至0.246。采用后验分析进行均衡比较。

Conclusion: 机器人运动能力对博弈学习有显著影响。DIC-Map能够有效分析并优化基于身体动态约束下的策略分配，替换估算器可大幅提升守门表现，体现身体约束与策略可学习性的深层耦合。

Abstract: Learning in robotic games is constrained not only by strategic information but also by what the body can still execute. We study this coupling in a hierarchical humanoid-quadruped penalty system in which game-level self-play policies command fixed soccer whole-body controllers (S-WBCs). The humanoid shooting skill is initialized from self-collected motion-capture data, whereas the quadruped saving skill is learned by reinforcement learning. We introduce dynamics-induced commitment mapping (DIC-Map), a body-grounded analysis that estimates continuation capability, identifies the first persistent loss of a terminal alternative, and tests whether the remaining interaction admits a reduced zero-sum game. For symmetric terminal alternatives, the reduced game yields a closed-form bound on optimal strategy concentration determined by the responder's value of deferring. We further show that, when the responder acts through an estimator, equal response values eliminate the direct terminal-allocation gradient and leave an estimator-mediated first-order learning channel. Experiments locate commitment about 0.29 s before contact, and changing only ball speed shifts deferral coverage. Across four responder policies, replacing the estimator raises save rate from 0.240 to 0.472, whereas a comparable gain in read accuracy obtained by waiting raises it only to 0.246. Posterior analysis is used for the equilibrium comparison because the available coverage terms are observational proxies. Project website: https://chris-ruizegeng.github.io/penaltykick/

</details>


### [158] [Learning Scene-Aware Humanoid Locomotion through 3D Clutter from Immersive Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21107)
*Beichen Wang,Tong Xu,Daniel Kosukhin,Yuen-Hei Yeung,Yuanjie Lu,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为MTC（Moving Through Clutter）的框架，用于提升人形机器人在复杂拥挤环境中的避障运动能力。通过虚拟现实数据采集、动作重定向算法和场景感知运动策略，显著提升了机器人多障碍物穿越的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人学习多为无障碍开放空间下的动态技能，对于三维、几何受限的拥挤环境下运动探索不足。复杂场景要求机器人全身动作与场景几何紧密耦合以实现高效避障。

Method: MTC框架基于从演示中学习。利用程序生成的虚拟现实环境收集人类在障碍环境下的自然运动数据，并提出场景感知动作重定向算法，将人类演示转化为适合机器人且严格避障的轨迹。之后，以这些轨迹训练Unitree G1人形机器人的场景感知运动策略。

Result: 在MTC提出的多障碍穿越测试（MTC-Challenge）中，该策略在多场景中平均达到70.2%的无碰撞通过率，展示出爬行穿越低矮通道、挤过狭窄缝隙等多样复杂全身技能。

Conclusion: MTC框架大幅提升了人形机器人在复杂拥挤三维环境中的穿越能力，为机器人高效安全避障运动提供了新途径。

Abstract: While learning from human motions has enabled highly dynamic humanoid skills such as dancing and martial arts in obstacle-free space, traversal through densely cluttered environments remains underexplored. These spaces are three-dimensional and geometrically constrained, requiring scene-aware locomotion that tightly couples whole-body motion with scene geometry for obstacle avoidance. To address these challenges, we present Moving Through Clutter (MTC), a learning-from-demonstration framework for scene-aware humanoid locomotion. To bypass costly physical scene construction, MTC uses procedurally generated Virtual Reality environments for immersive data collection. To transform these human motions into training-ready humanoid motions, we propose a scene-aware motion retargeting algorithm that converts human demonstrations into humanoid trajectories while strictly enforcing robot-scene clearance to guarantee collision-free traversal. These reference trajectories are then used to train a scene-aware locomotion policy that deploys on a Unitree G1 humanoid. Evaluated on our proposed MTC-Challenge for multi-obstacle traversal, the policy demonstrates a 70.2% collision-free rate across diverse scenarios, successfully traversing complex environments through diverse whole-body skills, including crawling through low-clearance passages and squeezing through narrow gaps.

</details>


### [159] [Demonstration Synthesis from a Single Scan via Gaussian Splatting for Visuomotor Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21112)
*Beichen Wang,Yuen-Hei Yeung,V. R. Sridhar Devarakonda,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的高保真数据引擎GaussianFactory，无需物理仿真器且仅需一次视频扫描，便可批量生成与目标环境高度匹配的视觉操作演示数据，大幅降低人力和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉操作策略的训练需大量与目标环境一致的演示数据，直接收集代价高昂，而合成方法则受限于人工繁琐、逼真度低或对物理仿真的过度依赖。

Method: GaussianFactory先用3D高斯喷溅重建场景为可编辑三维模型，再基于几何信息对任务进行运动学路径与抓取规划，渲染出逼真的演示视频。物理动态仅在抓取接触发生时引入，通过单次预训练的接触力模型进行预测，无需实时物理仿真。

Result: 基于合成演示训练标准扩散策略，在仿真场景与真实UR10e机器人上的成功率分别高达95.1%和84.2%。

Conclusion: GaussianFactory能以极低人工代价和高视觉保真度批量生成有效演示，显著提升示范合成的便捷性和训练下游策略的可行性。

Abstract: Training a visuomotor policy calls for abundant demonstrations that closely match the target environment, yet collecting them anew remains expensive. Existing demonstration synthesis methods reduce this cost but remain constrained by high manual effort, limited visual fidelity, or heavy reliance on physics simulators. This paper introduces GaussianFactory, a high-fidelity data engine that mass-produces demonstrations with a single video scan as its only human input and no physics engine in the generation loop. Specifically, GaussianFactory reconstructs the scene as an editable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) replica and samples from the object-combination tasks the scene affords. For each task, it plans grasps and trajectories purely kinematically on the geometric reconstruction, rendering photorealistic demonstrations that visually match the target environment. Physical dynamics enter the pipeline only where contact force interactions dictate the outcome---during grasp formation, via a learned contact model pretrained once on an interaction dataset. To evaluate the downstream utility of the synthesized demonstrations, we implement an end-to-end scan-to-deployment workflow in two setups: a simulated scene that stands in for the real world to enable reproducibility, and a real-world workspace with a physical UR10e robot. In each setup, a standard diffusion policy trained solely on the synthesized demonstrations achieves 95.1% and 84.2% success rates, respectively.

</details>


### [160] [Noctif3R: Feed-Forward Monocular Real-Time SLAM for Photon-Limited Scenes on Embedded Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21114)
*Mihir Chauhan,Aditya Uday Abhang,Kevin Biju Mathew,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于极低光照环境下单目RGB摄像头的机器人实时定位新方法SYS，并在多项极暗环境基准上超越现有主流SLAM系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在黑暗环境中，机器人定位面临实时性、计算资源有限和低信噪比等多重难题。虽然每项挑战单独已有方案，但几乎没有工作能实现在极暗、实时、低算力三者交汇下稳定工作。

Method: SYS是一套基于前馈点云地图并设有显式匹配门控的单目视觉SLAM流水线，兼顾快速重建与鲁棒性。系统在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin嵌入式平台优化，采用分辨率降采样、两项位姿优化改进，实现更高吞吐和更低能耗。

Result: 在九个极暗光等级基准场景下，SYS能在极低信号下输出有意义轨迹，优于DROID-SLAM、DPV-SLAM等主流方案。实验显示SYS出错率为最强对手的24%-47%，并显著降低GPU内存峰值与单位姿态能耗。黑暗实景实验表明，竞争方法在全黑帧仍输出无用姿态，SYS能准确停止。

Conclusion: SYS显著提升了极暗环境下的单目定位效果，兼具实用性和高效性，适合部署于功耗受限的移动机器人。

Abstract: Robots carrying out tasks in dark environments need to localize from a single RGB camera, in light so low that the per-pixel signal approaches the sensor's own noise, on a power-constrained onboard computer, in real time. Each of these constraints has matured pipelines, but the intersection does not. Offline low-light reconstruction now recovers structure below -4 dB but is far too slow to run in real time, while the real-time monocular systems a robot can actually carry (DROID-SLAM, DPV-SLAM, etc.) degrade or fail when SNR gets low. We measured how they fail: across the nine lowest darkness levels of our scenes, DROID-SLAM returns a full-length trajectory carrying no information about the camera's motion on all nine, VGGT-SLAM and CUT3R on eight, pi^3 on seven, and DPV-SLAM on four. We present SYS, a monocular pipeline built on a low-light feed-forward pointmap front end with an explicit match gate, which returns three tracked trajectories and no uninformative ones, at the lowest error of any method where it tracks (24-47% of the no-information ceiling against 56-73% for the strongest baseline), and at the narrowest coverage. On a real robot video take in which 86.5% of delivered frames are entirely black, every configuration of ours stops after the lit beginning, while DROID-SLAM and DPV-SLAM each emit a pose for all 1178 frames. Our method contribution is an embedded execution path for the Jetson AGX Orin: running the map, keyframes and backend at 384 pixels with tracking at 256, together with two fixes to the per-frame pose solve, is a replicated Pareto improvement, 1.28x throughput at 0.964x error on one scene and 1.42x at 0.68x on a second, with 47% less peak GPU memory and 29% less energy per pose. We evaluate on a calibrated, bit-exact regenerable noise ladder, on relabelled real-world dark exposures, and on a new dark-room video ladder recorded from a Boston Dynamics Spot robot.

</details>


### [161] [MetaPusher: Meta Learning and Planning for Nonprehensile Manipulation of Unseen Objects with Rapid Online Adaption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21122)
*Donghyung Lee,Seyedali Golestaneh,Jaskrit Singh,Zhuoyun Zhong,Athanasios Kapoutsis,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了MetaPusher框架，通过元学习和自适应规划，实现对未见过物体的非抓取操控任务，能够在任务执行中自适应动力学，无需单独收集数据即可提高操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法难以有效操控未见过的新物体，因为仅靠感知无法准确获取摩擦、质量分布等隐含物理属性；而通过以往经验获得的动力学模型在转移到真实环境时效果会下降。实现对新物体的高效操控，需要动力学的快速自适应及能动态吸收新信息的规划策略。

Method: MetaPusher采用元学习方法训练动力学模型，使其能够在操控新物体时凭借实时交互快速适应。配合自适应运动-动力学规划器，在任务过程中动态更新和优化规划搜索树，实现与动力学模型的协同进化。整个流程跳过了专门的数据收集阶段。

Result: 在仿真和仿真到真实的未见物体操控实验中，MetaPusher相较微调、主动学习、基于MPPI的控制和强化学习策略，动力学预测误差更低，任务成功率提升最高可达20%。

Conclusion: MetaPusher可高效对未见新物体实现无前期交互的数据自适应操控，提升了动力学模型的精度和实际操作成功率，为复杂动态转移问题中的机器人操控提供了新思路。

Abstract: Manipulating previously unseen objects remains challenging, as their dynamics depend on latent physical properties, such as friction and mass distribution, that cannot be inferred from perception alone. Prior experience across objects can provide an initial estimate of unseen object dynamics, but this estimate remains uncertain and can degrade further during sim-to-real transfer. Adapting the dynamics through interaction can progressively refine the estimation, however, updating the model may invalidate the planned trajectory. Successful and efficient manipulation therefore requires both rapid dynamics adaptation and a planning strategy that can incorporate this evolution. In this work, we introduce MetaPusher, a meta-learning and adaptive planning framework for nonprehensile manipulation of unseen objects without prior object-specific interactions. A meta-learned dynamics model rapidly adapts from interactions during task execution, while an adaptive kinodynamic planner updates long-horizon plans by reusing and refining its existing search tree. This coupling enables manipulation and adaptation without a separate data collection phase. We evaluate MetaPusher on unseen objects in simulation and in sim-to-real scenarios, comparing against fine-tuning and active learning methods, MPPI-based control, and a reinforcement learning policy. It achieves lower prediction error and improves task success rate by up to 20%.

</details>


### [162] [SAGE: Safety-Aligned Gradient Enforcement for Human--Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21130)
*Yisen Li,Hao Zhang,Ruize Geng,Yves Tseng,Ding Zhao,H. Eric Tseng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文针对多人-机器人协作中的可解释决策与安全约束两难问题，提出SAGE方法，并通过实验验证其有效性，显著减少碰撞和提升协作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协作型人机系统中，机器人既需决策透明可审计，又要执行符合安全物理约束的动作。现有方法将可解释策略与控制屏障函数结合，但在多主体强化学习中存在安全投影与团队优化不匹配的问题，影响系统性能和安全。

Method: 提出安全对齐梯度强化（SAGE）方法，内含屏障退火内化层（SAIL）和团队平均Lyapunov策略优化（TALO）。SAIL采用可微有限惩罚映射，执行时保持CBF二次规划，提升安全约束的内化响应。TALO通过Lyapunov修正校正独立参与者策略更新，以促进团队一致性。

Result: 在两台仿人机器人与一名人类合作实物实验，及九个仿真场景中，SAGE方法成功率71.0%，每千步平均碰撞0.5次。消融实验显示，直接CBF过滤能将碰撞频率降98.5%，但成功率略降为59.3%。SAIL能减少48.8%方案违约率和85.2%方案-执行差异，TALO降低50.8%更新一致性差距。

Conclusion: SAGE有效解决可解释决策与安全控制的两难，提升了多主体人机协作的安全性和性能，为实际部署打下基础。

Abstract: Multi-party human-robot collaboration poses a dual challenge: robot decisions should remain interpretable and auditable, while executed actions must satisfy safety constraints during physical interaction. Combining explainable decision-tree policies with control-barrier-function (CBF) filtering provides a promising architecture but creates two learning mismatches in multi-agent reinforcement learning. Safety projection changes the action applied to the environment, while the coupled proposal graph can misalign independently optimized actor updates with a team-level update. We present safety-aligned gradient enforcement (SAGE) to address both mismatches. Its shield-annealed internalization layer (SAIL) uses a differentiable finite-penalty proposal map while retaining the exact CBF quadratic program for execution, preserving constraint-normal sensitivity to internalize repeatedly active safety constraints. Team-averaged Lyapunov policy optimization (TALO) constructs a team-aware update reference and applies a Lyapunov half-space correction to regulate independent actor updates. Physical experiments with two humanoid robots and a human partner demonstrate deployment feasibility. Across nine simulation scenarios, SAGE achieves a 71.0% success rate with 0.5 collision steps per thousand environment steps. Ablations show that direct CBF filtering reduces collision frequency by 98.5% but decreases success from 67.3% to 59.3%. SAIL reduces proposal violation by 48.8% and proposal-execution correction by 85.2%, while TALO reduces the update-consistency gap by 50.8%.

</details>


### [163] [Diverse and Adaptable Arm Coordination for Octopus-Crawling via Diffusion-Based Uncertainty-Aware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21138)
*Seung Hyun Kim,Heng-Sheng Chang,Kimia Kazemi,Prashant Mehta,Mattia Gazzola*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种Diffusion-based Uncertainty-aware Optimization（DUO）算法，无需示范即可学习多臂软体机器人的多样化爬行控制器，提高其适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受章鱼爬行能力启发，软体机器人通过冗余实现多样化动作。但如何系统性发现和组织不同的协调模式以增强适应性仍具挑战。

Method: 提出DUO算法，将扩散式控制方法应用于模拟的多臂软体机器人。该方法采用具对称结构的控制策略表示，将多组相似控制器融合，并通过黑箱优化发现并保持多样化协调模式。同时提供策略编辑技术，可直接适应新的执行器约束。

Result: 在仿真环境下，章鱼机器人能够展现多样化运动行为，灵活应对动态物理约束，无需示范数据，实现多样协调策略的发现与应用。

Conclusion: 协调多样性的学习将运动冗余转化为软体多臂机器人适应性提升的有效资源，展示了DUO方法在无示范条件下实现高鲁棒性和适应性的潜力。

Abstract: Octopus crawling motivates soft robots that exploit redundancy, yet discovering and organizing diverse coordination modes for adaptation remains challenging. To address this, we introduce a Diffusion-based Uncertainty-aware Optimization (DUO) algorithm that learns demonstration-free crawling controllers for a simulated, muscle-actuated CyberOctopus. This work represents the first application of diffusion-based control to soft multi-arm robots in contact-rich simulations. By embedding a variety of locomotion behaviors within a shared control distribution, this approach enables the simulated octopus to navigate dynamic physical constraints, demonstrating that learned coordination diversity inherently facilitates robust adaptation. The main contributions include: (i) a symmetry-structured policy representation that folds radially equivalent controllers into a canonical directional sector, (ii) an online black-box optimization strategy, the DUO algorithm, that discovers and retains diverse coordination modes, and (iii) a control editing technique that adapts existing controllers to novel actuator constraints without retraining. These results show how learned coordination diversity makes motor abundance a practical resource for adaptation in soft multi-arm robots.

</details>


### [164] [Same World, Different Knowledge: When Isolated Audits Misjudge World-Model Repairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21155)
*Rui Min,Xianyao Li,Fang Xu,Sofiane Lachab,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文分析了信息路径对模型修复方案有效性的影响，提出了信息接口审计方法，并在四旋翼模型预测控制仿真中进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型修复通常仅考虑孤立输入故障，实际部署中多个模块可能共享错误信息，导致传统修复手段失效。论文希望更深入理解信息传递对修复效果的影响。

Method: 提出信息接口审计理论，将信息缺陷分为忠实度差距（精确输入变为估计）和可用性差距（输入丢失）；利用固定权重干预，测量预测误差、输入依赖性和成对闭环效益。以四旋翼模型为例，进行仿真实验分析不同校准错误及修复方法效果。

Result: 联合的相反符号10%质量/推力标定误差，会使物理指向模型的控制成功率从69%急降至8%；利用不确定性训练则能恢复至65%。风场重建虽恢复了控制能力但继承了误差依赖，正向校准偏移时不同修复方法间表现有反转。加速度诊断揭示了重建偏置与基础模型误差之间的补偿现象。

Conclusion: 修复手段的选择受限于实际部署时的信息路径。部分修复在孤立输入下有效，但当错误信息被共享时可能失效。因此，模型部署应注重信息接口审计，以合理制定修复策略。

Abstract: A repair favored under an isolated input fault can be inferior when deployed modules share the faulty information. We introduce an information-interface
  audit for world models, distinguishing fidelity gaps, where exact inputs become estimates, from availability gaps, where inputs are missing. Fixed-weight
  interventions measure prediction error, input dependence, and paired closed-loop benefit, including dependencies introduced by reconstruction. In
  simulated quadrotor model predictive control, coupled, opposite-sign 10% mass/thrust calibration errors reduce a physics-anchored model's success from 69%
  to 8%; uncertainty training restores 65%. Wind reconstruction recovers control benefit but inherits calibration dependence. For a positive calibration
  offset, reconstruction-only corruption favors uncertainty-trained reconstruction, whereas shared corruption favors the baseline. Acceleration diagnostics
  reveal compensation between reconstruction bias and nominal-model error, also observed with a disturbance observer. Repair selection therefore depends on
  the information paths used in deployment.

</details>


### [165] [MA-LIPP: Cooperative Multi-Agent Load-Aware Informative Path Planning for Heterogeneous Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21167)
*Hojune Kim,Guangyao Shi,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对外场机器人采样任务中，因载重增加所带来的路径规划难题，提出了多机器人协作的MA-LIPP方法，并通过理论与实验验证其高效与实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单机器人采样需频繁返回基站，导致空间覆盖受限，任务效率低。多机器人可分工协作，但协调递送任务带来新的调度难题。

Method: 提出MA-LIPP（多智能体负载感知采样与路径规划）框架，支持机器人异步“死信箱”传递样本，无需同步会合。将该问题建模为混合整数二次规划（MIQP），并提出可扩展的配对大邻域搜索（LNS）启发式算法以应对大规模场景。

Result: 所提LNS算法在95.5%的案例中达到最优解，大规模场景（高达12台机器人）下比顺序基线策略提高16.1%-19.8%的性能（加权后验方差下降）。

Conclusion: MA-LIPP及其高效求解方法为多机器人合作采样任务提供了有效范式，提高了采样效率，具有应用于复杂场景的潜力。

Abstract: Field robotics missions often require physical samples to be returned to laboratories for analysis, making path planning inherently load-aware and order-dependent as accumulated samples increase payload and traversal energy costs. In single-robot Load-Aware Informative Path Planning (LIPP), this rigidly couples sensing with hauling: a solitary robot must transport every collected sample, forcing frequent depot returns that severely restrict its spatial coverage. Heterogeneous multi-robot teams can overcome this bottleneck by dividing labor---enabling high-precision samplers to collect while high-capacity carriers handle transport. However, this introduces a complex coordination challenge regarding when, where, what, and to whom handoffs should occur on top of the LIPP problem. To address this tightly coupled problem, we introduce Multi-Agent LIPP (MA-LIPP), which enables teams to cooperate through asynchronous "dead drops," allowing one robot to deposit samples for another to retrieve later without requiring synchronous rendezvous. We formulate MA-LIPP as an exact Mixed-Integer Quadratic Program (MIQP) alongside a scalable Pairwise Large-Neighborhood Search (LNS) heuristic for complex real-world applications. The heuristic matches exact optima in $95.5\%$ of certified cases and reduces weighted posterior variance by $16.1$--$19.8\%$ relative to a sequential baseline on larger instances of up to 12 robots, providing a robust framework for cooperative physical-sampling missions.

</details>


### [166] [OpenRoIS: A Community-Driven Open-Source Middleware Implementing the Robotic Interaction Service (RoIS) Framework for Physical Robots and Virtual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21178)
*Sebastian Carrera Villalobos,Christopher Nolan Arellano,Arne Hitzmann,Edilson Morais Brito,Akira Utsumi,Yukiko Horikawa,Takahiro Miyashita,Lotfi El Hafi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoIS Framework 2.0 解决了人机交互软件受限于硬件平台的问题，但仅有标准规范并不足够。本文提出并实现了OpenRoIS——一个开源中间件，实现了RoIS标准，支持多语言（TypeScript、C#、Python）、多平台（物理机器人和虚拟代理）的互操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人机交互（HRI）服务应用通常只能针对特定硬件接口开发，硬件更换时需重写代码，极大限制了应用的可移植性和扩展性。

Method: 提出OpenRoIS，作为RoIS 2.0规范的开源实现。其架构包括可递归的引擎类别（主引擎与子引擎），内部组件与RoIS标准外部接口分离，采用JSON-RPC 2.0（WebSocket传输）实现接口映射，并通过统一类型管道生成多语言SDK，提供TypeScript、C#（支持Web和Unity）客户端SDK，以及支持ROS 2的Python适配器。

Result: 实现了可支撑物理机器人和虚拟代理的统一人机交互服务，可跨平台部署与开发，相关源码、接口定义和文档全部开源在 https://openrois.org/。

Conclusion: OpenRoIS不仅落实了RoIS标准，为实际开发提供完整工具链，还极大增强了人机交互服务的可移植性及社区参与度，有助于推动标准的落地应用。

Abstract: Service applications for human-robot interaction are commonly written against the hardware-specific interfaces of one platform, so a change of hardware forces a rewrite of the application. The Robotic Interaction Service (RoIS) Framework 2.0, standardized by the Object Management Group (OMG), addresses this fragmentation by defining a platform-independent model in which Service Applications interact with Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) Engines through standardized interfaces and hardware-independent symbolic messages. A specification alone, however, does not provide the maintained implementation, Software Development Kits (SDKs), and adapters needed for practical adoption. This paper presents OpenRoIS, a community-driven open-source middleware providing a concrete implementation of the RoIS Framework 2.0. It takes the position that an openly developed, paradigm-neutral implementation is what carries the standard from specification to practice. OpenRoIS contributes a recursive engine architecture in which a single engine class realizes the main and sub HRI Engine roles, an internal five-method component contract distinct from the five external RoIS interfaces, a mapping of those interfaces onto JSON-RPC 2.0 over WebSocket, a single-source-of-truth type pipeline that generates three consistent language stacks, TypeScript and C# client SDKs that include web and Unity support, and a Python adapter SDK that includes ROS 2 support. Through the common RoIS interfaces, a Service Application can address physical robots and virtual agents over the internet. All source code, interface types, and documentation are released under the Apache-2.0 license and openly developed at https://openrois.org/.

</details>


### [167] [When to Waddle: A Comparative Study of Bipedal Torso-Stabilization on Low-Friction Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21185)
*Naomi Oke,Ben Gu,George Ortiz,Stacy Ashlyn,Cordelia Pride,Sarah Bergbreiter,Aaron M. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究基于企鹅摆动步态，探索了躯干侧向运动和质心(COM)位置对双足机器人在不同地面摩擦系数下步态性能的影响，并通过仿真和实物测试验证了企鹅式步态在低摩擦下提升速度和能效的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双足行走在低摩擦表面面临较大挑战，常规直立步态容易打滑。企鹅在冰面上稳定行走，启发研究者模仿其躯干运动机制，探索应对低摩擦环境的新策略。

Method: 采用五驱动器双足机器人，在3D仿真平台MuJoCo中，分别测试传统直立步态和企鹅式“躯干靠上支撑腿”的步态，结合不同的质心布置和多种摩擦系数(mu=0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7)。随后在硬件系统中复现仿真实验，量化步行速度和运输能耗等指标。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在低摩擦下企鹅式步态产生更多成功步态控制器且前进速度更快，高质心配置表现最佳。实物实验同样证实低摩擦场景下企鹅步态和高质心提升了速度和能效。而在高摩擦(mu=0.45)下，低质心的传统步态反而速度和能效更佳，企鹅步态影响侧向摆脚，但对前进速度作用减小。

Conclusion: 躯干侧向运动和质心位置对步态性能的影响依赖于地面摩擦力。在低摩擦条件下，仿企鹅式躯干运动结合高质心设计显著提升速度与能效。企鹅步态为双足机器人适应滑面移动提供了新思路。

Abstract: Low-friction surfaces challenge bipedal locomotion by limiting the contact forces available during stepping. Inspired by penguin waddling, we investigate how lateral torso motion and center of mass (COM) placement affect locomotion as surface friction changes. Using a five-actuator biped, we compare an upright-gait strategy with a penguin-inspired torso-over-stance-leg strategy across multiple COM placements in simulation and hardware. In the 3-D simulator MuJoCo, we sweep through sinusoidal leg and hip actuation parameters across four friction coefficients mu = 0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7. In simulation, torso-over-stance-leg motion produces more successful controllers and higher forward speeds at low friction, with the highest speed occurring for the high-COM configuration. Hardware experiments show the same low-friction speed trend: at mu=0.12, torso-over-stance-leg motion increases forward speed and reduces cost of transport at both tested COM ratios, and the higher COM also improves both measures. The high-COM penguin configuration is the fastest and most energy efficient while maintaining low sideways foot motion. At mu=0.45, the COM trend reverses: the lower-COM configurations are faster and more energy efficient, while gait strategy has little effect on forward speed but still changes sideways foot motion. These results show that the effects of lateral torso motion and COM placement depend on the available friction, and that forward speed, energy use, and slip-related foot motion can be modulated with a penguin-inspired torso motion on hardware.

</details>


### [168] [Robust Structureless Monocular Visual Inertial Initialization Exploiting Line Features and Vanishing Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21186)
*Junwan Choi,Woongrae Jo,Dong-Uk Seo,Jinwoo Jeon,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SLIM-init的新型单目视觉-惯性里程计（VIO）初始化方法，通过利用2D线特征的几何约束，无需重建3D结构，提高了在退化运动下的初始化精度和鲁棒性。实验显示，SLIM-init在公开基准和挑战性场景中优于现有初始化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前视觉-惯性里程计（VIO）的初始化过程中，在退化运动（如低视差或平移主导等）时，通常需要特定激发运动或昂贵的3D重建，限制了其实用性和效率。因此，打破这一限制以提升初始化阶段的动作适应性和实用效率，是亟需解决的问题。

Method: SLIM-init方法无需显式3D点重建，通过直接利用跟踪到的2D线特征的几何约束来初始化VIO。具体包括利用线特征导出的灭点（VP）作为旋转约束，在退化运动下给出强鲁棒性的旋转信息；引入线的极线残差来约束平移，并采用线法向投影残差来提升对齐条件，最终整体提升初始化的鲁棒性和精度。

Result: 在公开基准数据集及自定义的挑战性退化运动序列上，SLIM-init在初始化精度与鲁棒性方面均优于当前主流VIO初始化方法，验证了其在实际应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: SLIM-init实现了结构无关、对退化运动高度鲁棒的VIO初始化，为高效实用的视觉-惯性系统部署提供了新的解决方案，具备广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate initialization is essential for reliable visual-inertial odometry (VIO), but it is often ill-conditioned under degenerate motions. Existing methods typically require restrictive excitation motions to ensure sufficient observability or rely on computationally expensive 3D structure reconstruction, limiting efficient and practical deployment. To address these limitations, we propose SLIM-init, a structureless monocular VIO initializer that directly exploits geometric constraints from tracked 2D line features without explicit 3D landmark reconstruction. Specifically, SLIM-init leverages line-derived vanishing points (VPs) as translation-invariant orientation cues to provide robust rotation-only constraints under degenerate scenarios such as low-parallax or translation-dominant motions. It further incorporates a line epipolar residual to constrain translation and a line-normal projection residual to improve the conditioning of linear alignment, enhancing the accuracy and robustness of initial state estimation. Extensive experiments on a public benchmark and challenging custom degenerate-motion sequences demonstrate improved accuracy and robustness over state-of-the-art initialization methods. The source code is available at: https://github.com/cjunwan/SLIM-init.

</details>


### [169] [Stochastic Neural Signed Swept Volume for Real-time Chance-Constrained Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21211)
*Qingyi Chen,Kevin Zhang,Lucas Chen,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种概率化模型来学习机器人运动轨迹的扫掠体的符号距离函数，可用于不确定环境中的高效安全运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统碰撞检测在连续轨迹上用离散点检查，依赖确定性环境模型，存在安全性和计算效率的权衡；连续方法成本高或者保守，数据驱动方法则容易出现近似误差，本质仅能作为初筛。

Method: 提出学习机器人扫掠体的Signed Distance Function（SDF）作为概率场，能表达模型不确定性、感知噪声，并能融合到几率约束轨迹优化框架。

Result: 方法在高维操作任务中进行验证，包括仿真和真实硬件，面对显著传感器噪声时也能有效工作。

Conclusion: 本方法能显式建模和利用不确定性，提高了高维场景下机器人的碰撞检测和运动规划的安全性与效率。

Abstract: Collision-free motion planning requires reliable collision models from sensed environments and validation of states along a continuous trajectory. To make this tractable, most planners check for collision at discrete states along continuous trajectories against a single determinized model of the environment, introducing a trade-off between safety and computational efficiency. While continuous collision checking approaches that approximate the swept volume of the robot exist, they are computationally expensive or overly conservative. Data-driven approaches can learn the swept volume; however, these neural models are susceptible to approximation errors and are therefore often limited to serving as coarse filters for downstream collision checkers. In this work, we propose to learn a signed distance function of the swept volume as a probabilistic field, enabling quantification of epistemic uncertainty, incorporation of perception noise, and eventual integration into a chance-constrained trajectory optimization framework. We demonstrate our approach on challenging high-dimensional manipulation problems with significant sensor noise, both in simulation and on real hardware.

</details>


### [170] [Visual Navigation Transformer with Pose Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21212)
*Beiming Li,Jaime Romero,Jonathan Diller,Vijay Kumar,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于视觉导航Transformer（VNT-PA）的导航策略，通过以位姿为索引的关键帧集合建模环境信息，实现了更高效和鲁棒的导航能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型导航策略依赖时序观测和位置编码，难以跨轨迹复用经验，而传统显式地图或图结构方法又导致规划效率低下。作者希望设计一种能直接复用不同轨迹空间观测经验的高效导航规划器。

Method: 提出了VNT-PA模型，将深度关键帧按相机位姿索引、作为上下文输入Transformer。利用位姿差异作为注意力权重，而非传统的时序顺序。该模型通过模仿基于真实三维场景网格的最短路径规划器进行训练，实现当前位姿和目标位置的动作预测。

Result: 在HM3D数据集的点目标导航任务中，VNT-PA取得93.3%成功率和90.4% SPL，显著优于基于时序编码或将位姿作为特征输入的基线方法。同时，该方法具有更高的训练效率，并在定位噪声下表现更稳健。

Conclusion: 以位姿索引的空间经验可直接作为导航规划的环境表征，用位姿差异驱动注意力机制可显著提升长距离导航的效果与效率，具有良好的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Learned navigation policies typically consume observations as a temporally ordered history, with positional encodings tying each observation to when it was seen, making it difficult to reuse experience from earlier traversals of an environment. Systems that do reuse such experience usually construct an explicit representation, such as a map or a topological graph, and plan on it. We propose VNT-PA (Visual Navigation Transformer with Pose Attention), a transformer planner whose context is a set of depth keyframes indexed by camera pose. With camera poses as positional encoding, attention depends on the pose differences between keyframes rather than on their temporal order. VNT-PA is trained to imitate a shortest-path planner operating on the ground-truth scene mesh, predicting actions by querying the spatial context with only its current pose and the goal position. On point-goal navigation in HM3D validation scenes, VNT-PA reaches 93.3% success and 90.4% success weighted by path length (SPL), outperforming baselines that encode the same context as a temporal sequence or treat pose as an input feature, in both navigation performance and training efficiency. Because the spatial context is a pose-indexed set, frames from different trajectories can be fused at test time. The planner also degrades more gracefully under localization noise than a conventional baseline which plans on explicit maps. These results show that pose-stamped experience can serve directly as the environment representation for a learned planner, and that making attention depend on pose differences, rather than on temporal order, speeds up training and improves long-horizon navigation.

</details>


### [171] [Fewer Steps, Better Actions: Rethinking Flow-Matching Inference for VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21216)
*Zhipeng Tang,Xinda Chen,Weining Rao,Xiao Li,Wenting Tan,Yuning Wang,Xiao Shi,Xiaofang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Coda的新方法，通过增加一次轻量的端点校正，提升了基于flow matching的视觉-语言-动作策略的效果，同时显著降低了推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作策略在执行时需要多次积分步骤以生成动作片段，虽然积分步数增加推理成本，但不一定能带来更高的任务成功率。因此，作者希望在提升性能的同时减少推理延迟。

Method: Coda方法先让一个冻结策略完成少量步数的噪声到动作轨迹，然后用轻量Transformer模型，结合候选动作、起始噪声及共享观测前缀缓存，预测一个端点残差校正。校正器仅通过行为演示进行训练，不需要重新训练主策略。

Result: 在50个RoboTwin Easy任务上，Coda在五步积分下成功率由71.64%提升到74.68%，推理延迟相较十步基线降低30.2%；二步积分配置下同样达到71.88%的成功率，实现了2.12倍速度提升。独立的13任务测试显示在几乎相同延迟下成功率提升5.69%；相同思路在官方SmolVLA冻结策略上也有类似提升。

Conclusion: 端点校正为静态flow-matching策略实现了更优的性能-延时权衡，是积分步数替代方案。这种方式提升了多种任务和系统的成功率，并降低了推理延迟。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies based on flow matching generate action chunks through repeated evaluations of an action expert. Increasing the number of integration steps raises inference cost, but does not necessarily improve closed-loop success. We propose Coda, which reallocates part of this integration budget to a single learned endpoint correction. A frozen policy first completes a few-step noise-to-action trajectory; a lightweight Transformer then predicts a demonstration-supervised residual using the candidate action, source noise, and shared observation-prefix cache. Only the corrector is trained. On 50 RoboTwin Easy tasks, five-step Coda improves success from 71.64% to 74.68% over the matched five-step baseline, while reducing forward latency by 30.2% relative to the default ten-step policy. A two-step configuration achieves 71.88% success with a 2.12$\times$ speedup. An independent 13-task control shows a 5.69-percentage-point gain at nearly equal latency, supporting correction as an effective alternative to additional integration. The same design also improves frozen official SmolVLA, raising two-step success from 60.8% to 69.4%. These results show that endpoint correction improves the quality-latency trade-off of frozen flow-matching policies.

</details>


### [172] [Safe Real-Time Policy Steering via Noise-Space Trajectory Optimization for One-Step Generative Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21220)
*Qingyi Chen,Joseph Ruan,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效的生成式机器人策略调控方法INSPO，通过在输入噪声空间进行轨迹优化，实现约束条件下的动作生成，兼顾多样性与实时性，效果优于传统采样和投影方法，且运行效率高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式机器人策略虽能表达多样化行为，但预训练策略难以适应部署时如避障等约束，且常用的推理阶段引导（如基于梯度的扩散或流迭代）计算量大，难以满足实时控制需求。因此，亟需高效、实时且能满足约束的策略调控方法。

Method: INSPO方法将生成策略的推理时引导问题，表述为在输入噪声空间的轨迹优化。通过优化输入噪声，使得生成动作在满足状态轨迹约束的前提下，不直接修改生成动作。方法采用正则项保持输入分布一致性，并使用基于种群的粒子优化在线求解。

Result: 在多种任务（包括状态/图像为基础的专用与通用视觉-语言-动作策略）和数据集上，INSPO在任务成功率与约束满足率上优于best-of-N采样及action projection，且在运行时延上优于梯度引导方法。

Conclusion: INSPO能够在不牺牲多样性的前提下，实现实时且具约束的生成式机器人策略引导，为机器人动态适应部署约束提供了一种高效实用的新方案。

Abstract: Generative robot policies can represent diverse, multimodal behaviors, but adapting pretrained policies to deployment-time constraints such as collision avoidance and orientation maintenance remains challenging. Existing inference-time steering methods typically apply gradient guidance through iterative diffusion or flow processes, which can be computationally expensive for real-time control. We propose INSPO, which formulates inference-time steering of one-step generative policies as trajectory optimization in the policy's input noise space. By optimizing the input noise while evaluating constraints on the induced state trajectory, INSPO searches the policy-induced behavior space without directly modifying generated actions. The optimization includes a regularization term that encourages solutions to remain consistent with the policy's input distribution and is solved online using population-based particle optimization. We evaluate INSPO on state- and image-based task-specific policies and generalist vision-language-action policies across Push-T, Can pick-and-place, and LIBERO-Spatial. INSPO improves task success and constraint satisfaction over best-of-N sampling and action projection, while comparing favorably with gradient-guided generation at lower runtime.

</details>


### [173] [SafeStage: Evaluating Safety Before, During, and After Vision-Language-Conditioned Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21223)
*Jinzhu Luo,Qi Zhang,Wei Wang,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeStage基准，用于全面评估视觉-语言控制机器人操作的安全性，涵盖任务前、中、后三个阶段，并揭示任务成功与安全违例常常并存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言机器人操作的评估主要集中在任务成功率或个别物理限制，缺乏对操作全流程安全风险的系统性分析，难以定位安全失效的具体阶段。

Method: 研究引入SafeStage基准，将机器人操作划分为初始状态风险、执行时安全和最终状态风险三个阶段，并设计97个特定风险场景进行测试，采用事件和状态检测方法独立评估每阶段安全性和任务完成度。

Result: 实验证明，视觉-语言机器人操作策略即使完成了任务，也常伴安全违例，不同策略在各阶段的失败类型不同。

Conclusion: SafeStage能有效分离任务成功与安全，定位违例发生阶段，为视觉-语言机器人操控提供了统一的诊断和改进平台。

Abstract: Vision-language-conditioned robot policies integrate perception, language understanding, and control for general-purpose manipulation. However, existing evaluations often focus on task success, isolated physical constraints, semantic refusal, or realized physical damage, providing limited insight into where safety fails during closed-loop manipulation. We introduce SafeStage, a lifecycle-structured benchmark for evaluating manipulation safety before, during, and after task execution. SafeStage contains 97 purpose-built risk scenarios organized into three stages. Initial-State Hazards captures safety-relevant relations that must be resolved before manipulating the target. Execution-Time Safety evaluates unsafe contacts, trajectories, region entries, and object interactions during execution. Final-State Hazards capture unstable or otherwise unsafe conditions remaining after nominal task completion. The benchmark evaluates realized interactions using event-based and state-based checks and reports native task success independently from stage-specific safety outcomes. We evaluate representative direct-action Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies and policies with world-model-based policies under a common closed-loop protocol. Our results demonstrate that nominal task completion frequently coexists with safety violations and that different policies exhibit distinct failure profiles across the three stages. By separating task success from safety and localizing when violations occur, SafeStage provides a unified diagnostic testbed for evaluating and improving vision-language-conditioned robot manipulation policies.

</details>


### [174] [AirSplan: Risk-Aware Motion Planning for Quadrotors in Cluttered 3D Gaussian Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21226)
*Seth Isaacson,William Hong,Katherine A. Skinner,Ram Vasudevan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于四旋翼无人机安全导航的新方法AirSplan，结合了3D高斯点云场景建模与基于可达性的运动规划器，能有效提升复杂环境下的安全通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼日益应用于农业、基础设施巡检等领域，这些任务场景复杂，对无碰撞导航的安全性要求极高，但环境信息常常不完整，现有方法难以兼顾场景表达精度和安全高效的路径规划。

Method: AirSplan采用标准化的3D高斯点云（Gaussian Splatting）对复杂场景结构进行高保真建模，无需地面真实障碍物数据。基于此，再结合一种新颖的可达性运动规划器，充分利用四旋翼的微分平坦特性，计算连续时间上的紧致碰撞约束，有效估算机器人占据空间，实现安全路径规划。

Result: 实验显示，AirSplan在81.2%的复杂测试案例中能够找到可行路径，显著超过现有最佳方法的51.2%。

Conclusion: AirSplan系统能在缺乏真实障碍物数据的复杂环境中高效、安全地为四旋翼规划路径，为提升无人机在现实世界复杂任务中的应用安全性开辟了新方案。

Abstract: Quadrotors are increasingly deployed in applications such as agriculture, infrastructure inspection, and maintenance. In each of these applications, the robot must navigate complex scene geometry while remaining strictly collision-free. Unlike in ground domains, even minor collisions for aerial vehicles can result in the loss of the robot. This safety requirement induces a pair of technical challenges. First, the environment must be represented with sufficient fidelity to encode complex structure, even when no ground-truth obstacle data is available. Second, a motion planner must leverage this representation to determine a collision-free path to the goal. This paper proposes a system that addresses these complementary challenges. The proposed method, AirSplan, adopts a normalized variant of 3D Gaussian Splatting that encodes high-fidelity scene geometry. It then applies a novel reachability-based motion planner that leverages the differential flatness of quadrotors to compute continuous-time collision constraints that tightly overapproximate the robot's occupancy. Experiments demonstrate that AirSplan successfully finds a path in 81.2% of challenging test cases, a significant improvement over the nearest baseline method's 51.2%.

</details>


### [175] [FOCAL-VLA: Subtask-Guided Geometry Distillation and Implicit World Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21228)
*Zhiyuan Gao,Di Wen,Yanxiang Zhan,Mohammad Khoshnazar,Jeroen Schäfer,Kunyu Peng,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为FOCAL-VLA的视觉-语言-动作模型框架，通过结合子任务引导的几何蒸馏和隐式世界建模，提升了机器人操作任务中的空间与动态理解能力，有效改善了精细及长时间操作任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在直接从2D观测映射到动作时，缺乏对场景空间和时间动态的深入理解，限制了其在复杂操纵任务中的性能。而现有改进方法由于引入全场景几何监督或未来状态预测，往往包含冗余信息，影响了对关键互动几何和动态的学习。

Method: FOCAL-VLA框架将与子任务相关联的几何知识进行蒸馏，通过将VGGT模型的几何潜变量与子任务相关区域的特征对齐，专注于当前子任务的空间结构学习。同时，引入Track4World的特征，实现对当前与未来互动动态的隐式三维建模。推理时无需运行VGGT和Track4World，利用这两类互补表示指导动作生成。

Result: 在模拟基准和真实世界操作任务中，FOCAL-VLA相较于现有方法表现更优，效果更突出。

Conclusion: FOCAL-VLA通过子任务导向的几何抽取与未来动态的隐式建模，有效提升了VLA模型的空间与时序理解，改善机器人复杂操控任务的能力，展示了其实用性和优越性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models built on pretrained vision-language models have demonstrated strong performance across diverse robotic manipulation tasks. However, VLA models that directly map current 2D observations to actions often lack sufficient spatial and temporal understanding, limiting their performance in precise and long-horizon manipulation. Recent methods enhance VLA models through geometric supervision and future-state prediction across the entire scene. However, these methods can suffer from redundant scene information, distracting the model from learning the geometry and dynamics relevant to the current interaction. To address this issue, we propose FOCAL-VLA, a framework that combines subtask-guided geometry distillation with implicit world modeling to learn representations of current spatial structure and future interaction dynamics. To focus geometric learning on the current subtask, we transfer geometric knowledge from VGGT to the VLA model by aligning geometry latents with features from subtask-relevant image regions. To capture the future 3D evolution of the current interaction, we incorporate implicit world modeling using Track4World features from current and future demonstration frames. The two complementary representations jointly guide action generation without running VGGT or Track4World at inference time. Experiments show that FOCAL-VLA outperforms baselines on both simulation benchmarks and real-world manipulation tasks. Project website: https://zhiyuan-gao.github.io/FOCAL-VLA/.

</details>


### [176] [KnowDemo: Knowledge-Guided Robot Demonstration Generation from Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21229)
*Zhiyuan Gao,Yanxiang Zhan,Mohammad Khoshnazar,Jeroen Schäfer,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出KnowDemo框架，通过从人类视频中提取结构化知识，自动生成多样性的机器人操作演示，实现更高效的数据收集和更强的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略学习依赖大量昂贵的演示数据；现有方法多仅参考运动轨迹，导致行为单一，且对任务理解不足，生成无效演示，提升空间有限。

Method: 提出KnowDemo，利用视觉-语言模型(VLM)从人类操作视频中提取对象描述、动作描述、任务条件等知识，并通过推理区分任务需求和演示细节，将知识映射到目标场景，对生成的候选方案做筛选和校验，再经过运动规划和仿真转化为可执行演示。

Result: 生成的演示具有多模态行为（不同接触策略、子任务顺序等），且带有结构化标签。实验显示，KnowDemo比单参考轨迹方法能发现额外的有效执行方式，并通过任务引导抓取采样，提升候选规划成功率。finetune下游策略模型实现了跨三项任务的sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: KnowDemo能高效生成结构化、多样化、有效的机器人操作演示，提升策略学习与迁移泛化能力，对现实机器人应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Learning robot manipulation policies typically requires substantial demonstration data, which are costly to collect on real robots. Recent methods generate robot demonstrations from human videos by adapting recovered motion and validating the resulting trajectories in simulation. However, methods centered on motion-reference adaptation can limit behavioral diversity by retaining the demonstrated contact strategies and subtask orders, while insufficient understanding of task requirements and scene relations can reduce demonstration generation efficiency by generating invalid candidates. To address these limitations, we propose KnowDemo, a framework that uses structured manipulation knowledge from human videos to generate diverse robot demonstrations for a target workspace. To distinguish task requirements from demonstration-specific choices, we develop a knowledge extraction and reasoning module based on a vision-language model (VLM) that associates object and action descriptions with inferred task conditions, demonstration references, and permissible execution variations. To translate this knowledge into executable demonstrations, we resolve the descriptions against target-scene entities and geometry to guide candidate generation and screening before motion planning and simulation. The resulting demonstrations exhibit multimodal behavior through alternative contact strategies and valid subtask orders, with structured execution labels. Experiments demonstrate additional verified execution modes beyond a reference-only configuration and improved candidate planning success through task-guided grasp sampling. To validate the generated data for policy learning, we fine-tune the pretrained $π_{0.5}$ model on simulation data, achieving sim-to-real transfer across three tasks. Project page: https://zhiyuan-gao.github.io/knowdemo/

</details>


### [177] [VLA-Scope: Shift-Aware Failure Prediction for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21246)
*Kaiwen Zhu,Dongfang Liu,Liangkai Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLA-Scope框架，通过结合输入分布偏移检测和执行历史，实现跨分布情形下机器人的故障预测。方法在十项任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型在分布外情形（OOD）下的稳定性不足，且单纯检测OOD输入无法有效预测执行失败。本研究动机是提高OOD下的机器人执行可靠性。

Method: 提出VLA-Scope双阶段方法：第一阶段利用图像和语言特征检测并分类分布偏移类别；第二阶段将分布偏移类别、动作前缀特征及执行进度特征输入到共享的逻辑回归模型，动态更新失败风险。

Result: 在LIBERO-Spatial数据集十项任务上，OOD检测ROC-AUC为0.9454，分布偏移分类准确率为91%。故障预测在1,400次OOD执行中ROC-AUC为0.8497，优于ActProbe与SAFE-MLP基线。

Conclusion: 结合动作和时序执行特征能提升输入分布偏移条件下的机器人故障预测效果，为提升模型稳健性提供新方向。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models map visual observations and natural-language instructions to robotic actions, but distribution shifts can compromise their reliability. Because these models may still succeed under out-of-distribution (OOD) conditions, detecting OOD inputs alone is insufficient to predict execution failure. In this paper, we introduce VLA-Scope, a two-stage framework that combines input-shift characterization with execution history to predict failure during OOD rollouts. The first stage uses pooled image and language representations to detect OOD inputs and classify their shift categories. For inputs flagged as OOD, the second stage combines the predicted category, action-prefix features, and execution progress features. A logistic regression model shared across shift categories updates failure risk as execution proceeds. We evaluate the framework with OpenVLA on ten LIBERO-Spatial tasks using leave-one-group-out cross-validation. OOD detection achieves a ROC-AUC of 0.9454, and shift classification achieves 91% accuracy. Evaluated independently of the OOD gate on all 1,400 OOD rollouts, the failure predictor achieves a ROC-AUC of 0.8497 after 60 executed actions, compared with 0.7906 without execution progress features. It also achieves a higher ROC-AUC than the evaluated ActProbe and SAFE-MLP baselines. These results suggest that combining action features with temporally aggregated execution step representations improves failure prediction under input shifts.

</details>


### [178] [LOInK: Learned Optimal Inverse Kinematics via Structured Neural Surrogate Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21275)
*Michael Somerfield,Damian Abood,Ruigang Wang,Ian R. Manchester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LOInK是一种通过学习获得逆向运动学问题近似最优解的新方法，利用神经网络学习空间映射与高效采样，能在多个实例中大幅提速并保证解的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆向运动学问题（IK）在机器人动作规划中非常常见，传统方法通常计算量大、求解效率低，需要更高效直接生成高质量IK解的新方法。

Method: LOInK方法通过训练获得配置空间到任务/潜变量空间的可逆bi-Lipschitz映射。潜空间结构化设计为使得代价最小化解集中于原点，利用基于operator splitting的网络反演算法能高效采样这些解。

Result: LOInK在三自由度机械臂、四足攀爬机器人和软体致动器的逆运动学任务上验证有效。其中对四足机器人，比传统约束优化“平均快31倍，最高100倍”，产生的解质量也更好。

Conclusion: LOInK能够高效生成高质量逆运动学近似最优解，尤其对复杂或需大量采样评估的任务具有明显提速和性能优势。

Abstract: We introduce Learned Optimal Inverse Kinematics (LOInK), a method to generate approximately optimal solutions to an inverse kinematics problem. When trained on data consisting of sampled configurations and associated task variables and a given cost function, LOInK learns a bi-Lipschitz invertible mapping from configuration space to a decoupled task/latent space, and moreover, the latent space is structured so as to place cost-minimizing solutions at the origin. This enables efficient sampling of cost-minimizing solutions via a network-inversion algorithm based on operator splitting. We demonstrate the proposed approach on three problems: an illustrative three degree-of-freedom manipulator problem; a quadrupedal climbing robot for which LOInK can generate near-optimal solutions on average 31 times faster and up to 100 times faster than a constrained optimization approach; and a simulated soft actuator as a purely data-driven example, in which LOInK can explicitly generate high-quality solutions, unlike existing generative approaches that require diverse sampling and evaluation of candidate solutions.

</details>


### [179] [Stability-aware Residual Reinforcement Learning Framework for Robotic Manipulator Disturbance Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21307)
*Jihong Kim,Joonhyuk Kwon,Hwa Soo Kim,TaeWon Seo,Hyung-Tae Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种残差强化学习扰动观测器（DOB）框架，通过结合分析型观测器与强化学习策略，实现机械臂更高精度跟踪和更强的扰动适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制器和扰动观测器虽为机械臂高精度控制标准方法，但面对参数不确定性、非线性摩擦和复杂扰动时，性能受限。因此，需要新的方法以提升鲁棒性和精度。

Method: 提出了一种残差强化学习扰动观测器框架，将分析型观测器（处理已知部分）与强化学习策略（补偿模型无法捕捉的残差）相结合。引入估算器网络，通过观测历史与特权扰动上下文对齐，使补偿操作对扰动具有感知能力，并按扰动模式组织潜在空间。采用状态相关的动作约束（由输入到状态稳定性理论推导），保证闭环系统跟踪误差最大限度受控。

Result: 在6自由度机械臂上进行实验验证，实现了扰动估计和轨迹跟踪能力的一致提升。真实硬件零样本迁移实验下，跟踪误差降低27.8%；在未见过的基座振动扰动下，误差降低38.0%。

Conclusion: 所提方法显著提升机械臂对未知和复合扰动的适应性及跟踪精度，且具有理论稳定性保证，在实际硬件上展示了优越的效果与迁移能力。

Abstract: Although conventional controllers and disturbance observers (DOBs) are the standard for precision tracking in manipulators, they suffer from parameter uncertainty, nonlinear friction, and compound disturbances. This study proposes a residual reinforcement learning DOB framework that pairs an analytical observer with an RL policy. The deterministic baseline operates within a reliable region, whereas the RL policy explicitly targets the residuals that the model cannot capture. To make this compensation disturbance-aware, an estimator network aligns the observation history with a privileged disturbance context, organizing the latent space by disturbance regime and enabling rapid adaptation across disturbance transitions. To guarantee stability, we derived and enforced a state-dependent action bound on the RL policy from an input-to-state stability (ISS) analysis such that the closed loop provably confines the tracking error to a certified envelope for arbitrary policy outputs. Experiments on a 6-DOF manipulator demonstrated consistent improvements in disturbance estimation and tracking, including a 27.8% tracking-error reduction on real hardware under zero-shot sim-to-real transfer and a 38.0% reduction under a base-vibration disturbance that was not observed during training.

</details>


### [180] [NaViRrator: Robot Navigation from Human-Readable Maps through a Learned Visual Route](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21316)
*Ayun Lee,Jiseon Kim,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了NaViRRator框架，实现从人类可读地图上的起止点生成导航指令，显著提升了机器人导航的成功率和路径效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可读地图易于为机器人导航指定目标，但如何将地图上的信息转换为机器人所需的指令，并与机器人当前所见结合，依然困难。既有方法在地图与语言、指令之间的无缝对接上存在准确率和灵活性不足，亟需新的桥接方式。

Method: 提出RouteScribe方法将路径推断与语言描述分离，首先用SGL-CFM方法在地图坐标上生成路径骨架，然后用预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）将其转化为导航指令。导航执行时，机器人仅依赖该指令和自身视角，无需依赖地图或骨架，实现模块解耦。

Result: 实验证明，该方法导航成功率和路径效率（SPL）均优于直接地图到指令、基于A*的骨架与高斯源方法。定性分析也显示路径指令更清晰、动作顺序保留得更好。

Conclusion: NaViRRator框架和RouteScribe方法有效提升了地图到导航指令的转换质量，为人类可读地图和预训练导航策略间提供了更清晰可替换的接口，对实际机器人导航系统具有重要意义。

Abstract: Human-readable maps provide an intuitive interface for specifying robot destinations, but connecting their schematic geometry to egocentric observations remains challenging. We present NaViRRator, a framework that translates user-specified start and goal locations on such maps into navigation instructions for a pretrained vision-and-language navigation (VLN) policy. Its core method, RouteScribe, separates route inference from verbalization by first generating an explicit route scaffold in map-image coordinates, which a pretrained vision-language model (VLM) converts into a navigation instruction. We construct the scaffold with start--goal line conditional flow matching (SGL-CFM), which deforms a straight start--goal waypoint sequence into a map-conditioned route. During execution, the VLN policy receives only the instruction and egocentric observations, while the map and scaffold remain upstream, allowing executor replacement without retraining the map-to-language modules.
