Real-world experiments show higher success rates and success weighted by path length (SPL) than direct map-to-instruction generation, A*-based scaffolding, and Gaussian-source conditional flow matching. Qualitative results further show clearer salient turns and better preservation of the intended maneuver sequence, supporting route-grounded language as a modular interface between human-readable maps and pretrained navigation policies.

</details>


### [181] [LEMCA: LLM-Guided Synthesis of Efficient Mode-Switching Control Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21319)
*Arjun Krishna,Vincent Pacelli,Dinesh Jayaraman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用大语言模型（LLM）引导自动生成高效模式切换控制架构（LEMCA）的方法，在保证性能的同时，实现了资源的动态高效利用。实验显示，LEMCA在多个任务上优于传统一体化控制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的模式切换控制器设计需要专家手工编写，效率低下，难以适应不同新任务。为避免复杂设计，现有主流机器人控制常采用一刀切的方案，严重浪费资源并且效率低下。

Method: 提出LEMCA方法，通过LLM的语义推理和编码能力，自动编辑和优化控制模式、资源分配及切换策略，将控制架构表示为可解释的程序形式，并结合强化学习等优化方法评价和进化控制方案。

Result: LEMCA在多种物理控制基准任务中，稳定找到优于“一体化”设计的新策略，在容易阶段实现资源回收，整体超越了传统方案的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: LEMCA为自动且高效的模式切换控制器设计提供了一条低门槛的新路径，使得在保持性能前提下实现资源优化，具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: Physical control tasks in the natural world, such as driving or object manipulation, frequently exhibit dramatic variations in sensory and compute complexity over time. Correspondingly, a natural resource-efficient choice for robot control is to dynamically switch between control modes with varying resource allocations. However, such "mode-switching controllers" (MSCs) have historically required laborious, expert-driven design and synthesis for each new task. Driven by these design difficulties, modern robotic control architectures often fall back to a wasteful "monolithic" one-size-fits-all structure, where resource allocation is permanently anchored to the hardest, most resource-intensive task phases. To facilitate the design of performant yet efficient MSCs, we propose LLM-Guided synthesis of Efficient Mode-Switching Control Architectures (LEMCA). LEMCA represents MSC designs as interpretable programs to be iteratively refined in an evolutionary loop. To evaluate design fitness, we propose MSC-compatible extensions of automated controller synthesis approaches, such as reinforcement learning in simulation. LEMCA then leverages the semantic priors, reasoning, and coding capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) to iteratively edit controller modes, their corresponding sensory-compute resource allocations, and mode transitions. Our experiments across diverse control benchmarks show that LEMCA consistently discovers strategies that surpass the Pareto frontier of monolithic designs by reclaiming wasted resources during "easy" task phases. LEMCA thus presents an automated, low-effort path to synthesize resource-efficient MSC designs.

</details>


### [182] [The EventCV Library for Event-Based Robotic Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21330)
*Adam D. Hines,Michael Milford,Tobias Fischer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了EventCV，这是一个用于事件相机的开源库，功能丰富且易于集成，能高效处理事件流并支持各类机器人应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管事件相机在高动态范围、低延迟与低功耗方面对机器人具有显著优势，但其集成应用仍面临：缺乏即插即用驱动、数据格式不统一和代码标准化不足等挑战。为降低入门门槛、促进事件相机在机器人领域的实际应用，需开发更为强大便捷的工具链。

Method: 作者开发了EventCV，一个以Rust实现、具备OpenCV风格Python绑定的开源库。功能包括：去噪滤波、几何变换、数据增强、角点检测、无监督特征学习、基于对比度最大化的运动估计、视频转事件仿真、以及ONNX推理等。其集成Neuromorphic Drivers，可实现实时事件流处理。

Result: EventCV可解码和建立表示的速度比现有库快1.1至3.7倍，功能覆盖面也更广。已在Jetson Orin AGX上部署，并完成三项机器人案例分析，涉及目标检测、端侧模型推理和定位。

Conclusion: EventCV极大简化了事件相机在机器人中的应用流程，具备实时高效和功能丰富的优点，为事件驱动视觉和机器人实际落地提供了强大支撑。

Abstract: Event cameras detect per-pixel brightness changes asynchronously on microsecond timescales, with high dynamic range and low power draw. These are desirable properties for robots that move fast or work in difficult lighting conditions. However, integrating an event camera into a real-world robotic pipeline still requires substantial effort: plug-and-play drivers do not exist, event streams are recorded in a variety of incompatible file formats, and most projects rely on custom research-grade code. Here, we present EventCV, an open-source and extensible Rust library with OpenCV-style Python bindings that lowers the entry barrier to working with event cameras. EventCV provides a wide range of features: denoising filters and geometric transforms, augmentations, corner detection and unsupervised feature learning, contrast-maximization motion estimation, a video-to-events simulator, and Open Neural Network Exchange (ONNX) inference for deployment in robotic stacks. EventCV integrates the Neuromorphic Drivers package, allowing an event camera stream to be processed directly in real time. No existing toolkit covers this range of operations in one package, and EventCV builds representations and decodes files 1.1x to 3.7x faster than the currently available libraries. We deploy EventCV on a Jetson Orin AGX and present three robotics case studies spanning object detection, on-device model inference, and localization. Project webpage: https://eventcv.net.

</details>


### [183] [FAN: Foresight Action Normalization for Continual Adaptation of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21358)
*Yijun Hong,Jiarun Zhu,Xiaoquan Sun,Le Xu,Qijun He,Xin Jin,Mingqi Yuan,Wenjun Zeng,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五种动作归一化策略在多种视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型持续学习场景下的表现，并提出了一种新策略FAN，显著提升了长期任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽在多任务操作上表现优异，但持续学习环境下容易因坐标漂移、动作覆盖不足等问题丢失性能，归一化机制常被忽视。作者希望解决动作表示稳定性问题，以实现高效的持续学习。

Method: 作者系统比较了五种动作归一化方法，在涵盖单双臂操作的四大任务序列上实验，揭示现有方法缺陷。随后根据一致性、覆盖性和因果性三原则(3C)提出FAN（Foresight Action Normalization），该方法用小规模、任务无关的数据集预先计算归一化参数，并在后续学习阶段保持冻结。

Result: FAN方法在所有实验任务流中表现最优，显著提升了持续学习中的稳定性和鲁棒性，克服了传统策略的漂移和失配问题。

Conclusion: FAN为实现稳健、有效的终身VLA模型持续学习提供了新思路，强调设计合理动作归一化对多任务机器人的重要性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models pre-trained on large-scale, closed datasets have demonstrated remarkable success across diverse robotic manipulation tasks. However, their long-term real-world deployment necessitates continuously acquiring new skills while retaining previously learned capabilities. While pioneering works have explored continual VLA adaptation using techniques such as experience replay and reinforcement fine-tuning, they overlook a foundational mechanism: action normalization, which determines the underlying coordinate system in which policies perceive and execute physical actions. To bridge this gap, we systematically evaluate five normalization strategies across four real-world task streams covering single-arm and bimanual manipulation. Our analysis reveals that existing protocols induce severe failure modes due to inter-task coordinate drift, limited motion coverage, or train-test coordinate mismatches. Motivated by these insights, we formulate three core design principles: consistency, coverage, and causality (3C), and introduce foresight action normalization (FAN). FAN estimates normalization statistics once from a small, task-independent calibration set prior to continual learning and freezes them throughout adaptation. Across all evaluated streams, FAN achieves the highest performance and demonstrates consistent robustness, providing insightful guidance for building stable action representations in achieving effective lifelong VLA adaptation.

</details>


### [184] [MicroHookACT: Monocular Microscopic Vision Guided Visuomotor Policy for Flexible Microelectrode Hooking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21365)
*Yitong Chen,Fangbo Qin,Yang Wang,Ruihua Hu,Kui Zhang,Shan Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了MicroHookACT，一种基于模仿学习的视觉—动作策略，用于在单目显微视觉下实现柔性微电极植入中的自动3D穿线钩挂。通过实验验证，该方法在不同难度设置下整体成功率达到96.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性微电极植入手术需要高度精确的自动穿线钩挂操作，但现有方法在显微视觉和动态变化环境下难以实现稳定高效的控制。

Method: 1. 提出单向钩挂策略，利用离焦线索与光轴引导实现不需触诊的精确对准和穿线。2. 基于冻结ViT骨干的动作监督型目标注意模块，通过人类示范直接学习关注微针尖和微环，无需人工标注。3. 采用动态加权的全局粗与局部细特征以适应操作全过程中的离焦模糊和视觉需求变化。训练集采用60组人类演示，评估包括五种不同难度设置。

Result: MicroHookACT在全部测试条件下取得最高96.7%的成功率，平均执行时间为11.5秒，显著优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了通过模仿学习获得的视觉—动作策略在多变显微操作环境下实现微米级精确操控的可行性和优越性能。

Abstract: Automated needle-loop hooking is a critical step in flexible microelectrode (FME) implantation. This paper presents MicroHookACT, an imitation learning-based visuomotor policy for automated 3D hooking under monocular microscopic vision. First, a unidirectional hooking strategy exploits defocus cues and optical-axis guidance to enable palpation-free precise alignment and contact-rich threading. Second, an action-supervised object attention module built on a frozen ViT backbone learns to focus on the micro-needle tip and micro-loop directly from human demonstrations, without requiring manual visual annotations for training. Third, attention-centered global coarse and local fine features are dynamically weighted according to predicted action progress, enabling a single ACT policy to adapt to changing defocus blur and visual requirements throughout the operation. In the experiments, visuomotor policies were trained on 60 human demonstrations and evaluated under five setups with varying difficulties. Our MicroHookACT framework achieved the highest overall success rate of 96.7\% with an average execution time of 11.5 s. These results demonstrate the potential of visuomotor policy learning for micron-level control under varying operating conditions.

</details>


### [185] [ProTracer: Proprioception-Guided Failure Diagnosis in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21369)
*Chang Dong,Mehdi Hosseinzadeh,King Hang Wong,Lingqiao Liu,Francois Fraysse,Feras Dayoub,Minh Hoai Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ProTracer框架，实现机器人的多维度失败分析，包括检测、归类、定位失败发生的确切时间，并能用自然语言生成失败解释。该方法无需额外训练，集成视觉-语言模型(VLM)及本体感知信号。引入FailTime基准集，实验验证该框架在多项任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人任务失败分析常忽略失败发生的精确时刻，同时缺乏能够同时融合多模态信息且易用的分析框架。作者希望提升对失败细节、时序和机制的辨析能力，助力机器人系统鲁棒性提升。

Method: 提出ProTracer框架：1) 利用本体感知动态信号提取动作边界，实现精确的时序分析；2) 将丰富的机器人状态信号转为结构化自然语言描述，与视觉观测一起送入现有视觉-语言模型（VLM）进行多模态联合推理；3) 无需训练新模型。此外，构建了FailTime评测集，用于同步测试视觉和本体感知下的多种失败分析任务。

Result: ProTracer在传统失败检测、分类、解释任务以及新提出的失败发生时刻定位任务上均取得了强有力的性能，实验证明其准确性和实用性优于以往方法。

Conclusion: proprioceptive信号有效提升了机器人失败分析的时序精度，ProTracer框架能够高效、无监督地融合多模态信息，推动机器人细粒度失败分析迈上新台阶。

Abstract: This paper presents a comprehensive framework for robot manipulation failure analysis that includes binary failure detection, failure categorization, explanation generation, and the additional capability of failure onset localization, which aims to identify the earliest moment at which a robot execution deviates from a valid task-completion trajectory and is ultimately followed by task failure. To address these tasks, we propose ProTracer, a training-free framework that leverages existing Vision-Language Models (VLMs) together with proprioceptive signals for failure analysis. Our method uses proprioceptive dynamics to identify temporally informative action boundaries and converts richer robot-state signals into structured natural-language descriptions that can be jointly analyzed together with visual observations by the VLM. This design combines the temporal precision of proprioceptive signals with the multimodal reasoning capabilities of modern VLMs without requiring additional model training. We further introduce FailTime, a benchmark with synchronized visual and proprioceptive observations for evaluating conventional failure diagnosis tasks as well as failure onset localization. Experiments demonstrate that ProTracer achieves strong performance across both conventional failure diagnosis tasks and the newly introduced failure onset localization task, highlighting the importance of proprioceptive reasoning for fine-grained temporal failure analysis.

</details>


### [186] [AVT-Fabric: Active Visuo-Tactile Perception via Adaptive Evidence Selection for Efficient Robotic Fabric Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21377)
*Chang Gao,Zhuo Chen,Suhang Xia,Jihong Zhu,Jiankang Deng,Shan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AVT-Fabric，一个结合视觉与触觉信息、用于织物对比的新框架，能自适应选择触觉证据，从而提升机器人对织物的理解效率和准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人织物对比领域，单一视觉或触觉信息往往不足以精准区分不同织物，如何高效组合两类感知成为关键。

Method: AVT-Fabric采用以RGB视觉为主导，借助双尺度门控机制，根据模型的信心度适时触发GelSight触觉采集，同时以紧凑的文本记忆记录对比过程，通过多数投票集成多次决策。对5阶段流程平均只用1.6次，极大提高计算效率，并支持多种MM-LM骨干网络。

Result: 在400组测试中，AVT-Fabric用7B参数模型达到98.0%准确率，比90B参数的基线高4个百分点，推理延迟减少61.8%；实机部署中对织物排序准确率78.1%，7/8场景下成功完成识别。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了自适应感知证据选择显著提升了机器人多模态感知的准确性与效率，对实际应用具有广泛推广价值。

Abstract: Robotic fabric comparison needs to actively combine visual appearance and tactile cues. Here, we present AVT-Fabric, an RGB-first framework that allocates tactile evidence according to the difficulty of each comparison. A dual-scale gate evaluates answer-token confidence and raw logit separation to determine whether another force-tagged GelSight observation is needed. Compact textual memory preserves the executed history, and majority voting consolidates the selected predictions. On 400 held-out comparisons, AVT-Fabric achieves 98.0% accuracy with a compact 7B Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM), surpassing the 94.0% reported by the 90B MLLM-Fabric baseline by 4.0 percentage points while processing only 1.60 of five available stages on average. It improves on matched passive inference by 9.25 percentage points and reduces model-side latency by 61.8%, while also improving on RGB-only accuracy. Four additional MLLM backbones support the generalizability, accuracy, and efficiency of the framework. This framework is also deployed on a real robotic system, achieving 78.1% pairwise ranking accuracy and correct fabric selection in seven of eight application scenarios. AVT-Fabric demonstrates that adaptive evidence selection can improve both the accuracy and efficiency of robotic visuo-tactile reasoning.

</details>


### [187] [MarineCraft: Enabling Rapid Prototyping of Underwater Robots via Modular Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21396)
*Yuta Sugiura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MarineCraft是一种模块化开发工具包，通过消除防水和布线的复杂性，极大加速了水下机器人的快速原型开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人的开发受限于防水和密集布线的难题，导致原型设计周期长、效率低，因此需要创新方法来加快开发进程。

Method: 提出了MarineCraft工具包，具备自包含的防水推进模块，将动力、无线通信和驱动器集成在一起，无需集中布线和重复密封。模块化设计支持快速组装和结构重构，数分钟内即可实现新的机器人几何结构。

Result: 实验证明该模块化系统支持2.5米水深下的可靠、无渗漏工作，能显著加快原型开发和设计迭代速度。

Conclusion: MarineCraft通过模块化和结构重构大幅降低了水下机器人开发门槛，是实现快速物理原型制作的有效工具。

Abstract: Underwater robot development is often hindered by the complexities of waterproofing and wiring, which significantly delay the rapid prototyping process. This paper presents MarineCraft, a modular toolkit designed to accelerate the development cycle through structural reconfiguration. The system features self-contained, waterproof propulsion modules that integrate power, wireless communication, and actuation. By eliminating centralized wiring and the need for repeated sealing, MarineCraft allows diverse robot geometries to be assembled and tested in minutes rather than days. Experimental results demonstrate that this reconfigurable architecture enables fast, iterative design cycles while maintaining reliable operation and leak-free performance at depths of up to 2.5 meters. Our toolkit effectively lowers the barrier to underwater robotics by transforming modularity into a vehicle for rapid physical prototyping.

</details>


### [188] [Stabilizing Trajectory Outputs in End-to-End Autonomous Driving via SC-IMM Based Teacher Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21404)
*Siewoo Kim,Seung-Hyun Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种将场景条件交互多模型（SC-IMM）机制用于端到端自动驾驶模型中，以生成离线教师信号，从而提升轨迹输出的稳定性。实验在CARLA虚拟环境中显著提升了驾驶表现、减少了碰撞并改善了轨迹平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于路标的模仿学习方法仅最小化轨迹坐标误差，难以反映场景相关的路径速度变化，导致路径输出在不同场景下不稳定。

Method: 作者提出将专家轨迹转化为路径-速度状态，并用SC-IMM（基于场景线索更新的交互多模型）更新，生成包含路径-速度标签和模型后验概率的教师信号。这些信号作为辅助监督项加入原有轨迹损失中进行训练，推理架构和控制器保持不变。

Result: 在CARLA Town12的100条短路线闭环测试中，相比基线方法，驾驶得分提升28.0%，每公里碰撞数降低62.3%，轨迹平滑性指标也得到改善。

Conclusion: 嵌入场景条件运动模型线索的离线教师信号能有效提升自动驾驶轨迹输出模型的稳定性和安全性。

Abstract: End-to-End autonomous driving models commonly predict future waypoints from sensor inputs and convert them into vehicle control commands through a downstream controller. However, conventional waypoint-based imitation learning mainly minimizes coordinate-level errors, making it difficult to capture scene-dependent path-speed changes and temporal instability across waypoint outputs. In this paper, we propose an offline teacher-signal generation and learning method for trajectory-output stabilization based on a Scene-Conditioned Interacting Multiple Model (SC-IMM) to mitigate this issue. The proposed method converts expert trajectories into path-speed states and performs IMM updates conditioned on scene cues to generate path-speed teacher labels and mode posterior probabilities. The generated signals are added to the original trajectory loss as auxiliary supervision during training, while the inference structure and waypoint controller remain unchanged. In closed-loop evaluation on 100 short routes in CARLA Town12, the proposed method improved the driving score by 28.0% and reduced Collision/km by 62.3% compared with the baseline, while also improving jerk and trajectory-variation metrics. These results demonstrate that offline teacher signals embedding scene-conditioned motion-model cues can guide trajectory-output driving models toward more stable closed-loop behavior.

</details>


### [189] [A Unified Dynamic Force Guidance Framework for Performance-Optimized Kinesthetic Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21416)
*Chunxin Li,Jianhua Wu,Zhenhua Xiong,Xiangyang Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在线动态力引导方法，在协作机器人示教过程中优化机器人性能，通过虚拟力帮助用户拖动机器人到更优配置，从而提升机器人的运行性能和工作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作机器人在工业领域频繁换型时，需快速部署。传统的示教方法（如手势拖拽）虽然直观，但容易因忽视机器人运动性能导致性能下降，影响实际运行效率。为解决这个问题，作者致力于在示教时实时保障和提升机器人的运动性能。

Method: 提出了结合性能约束与优化机制的动态力引导方法。采用可变顺应控制，确保机器人的运动性能（以Jacobian矩阵最小奇异值为指标）高于给定阈值。系统还引入主动虚拟力，引导操作者将机器人拖向性能更好的配置，并在6自由度协作机器人上进行了典型路径实验。通过轨迹回放对比分析性能与效率。

Result: 实验对比了三种典型路径，显示提出的方法能有效提升机器人运动性能。轨迹回放实验表明，改进的示教路径提升了机器人的工作效率。

Conclusion: 该方法可在线提升协作机器人示教过程中的运动性能与工作效率，有助于工业场景下缩短生产节拍，具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Collaborative robots are increasingly deployed in industrial scenarios characterized by frequent product changeovers. As an intuitive programming method, kinesthetic teaching facilitates rapid robot deployment. However, users may overlook the configuration of the robot during kinesthetic teaching, leading to degradation in operational performance. Operational performance refers to the capability of the robot to generate motion and can be quantified by the Minimum Singular Value of the Jacobian matrix. To address this issue, this paper proposes an online dynamic force guidance method that integrates performance constraint and optimization mechanisms. Specifically, variable admittance control maintains the operational performance of the robot above a predefined threshold, while a virtual force actively guides the user to drag the robot towards configurations with improved performance. Experiments are conducted on a 6-DOF collaborative robot, comparing three typical paths in the task space. To evaluate the quality of the taught trajectories, trajectory playback experiments are conducted to analyze the relationship between the operational performance of the robot and the work efficiency. The results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively enhances the operational performance of the robot and consequently improves the work efficiency, holding significant value for reducing production takt time in industrial deployment.

</details>


### [190] [FootQuery: Future-Touchdown-Guided Retrieval from Depth History for Perceptive Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21447)
*Tao Dong,Jia Yu,Yuxuan Fan,Linna Zhao,Jiaqi Gong,Andong Yang,Chao Gao,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FootQuery的新型人形机器人行走感知框架，通过基于预测落足点查询历史深度图像，实现复杂地形下的稳定行走。相比已有方法，该方法在仿真和现实测试中展现出更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在复杂地形上行走时，落足点往往在落地前已不可见，受限于摄像头视野和自遮挡。因此需要有效利用过往观测信息，提升机器人的感知和落足预判能力。

Method: FootQuery方法利用机器人自身感知预测下一个足部落地点及其不确定性，并基于此从历史稀疏采样的深度图像中针对每只脚检索关联特征。训练阶段通过将实际接触点投影回历史图像进行监督。检索到的足部特征还与全局视觉记忆融合生成控制动作。训练中采用渐进力辅助和接触一致性方法，增强模型探索性和协调性。

Result: 在仿真测试中，FootQuery框架在最具挑战性的台阶、缺口、平台等地形上均优于被移除部分组件的对照方法。现实世界实验中，在Unitree G1机器人上实现了同一策略跨多种地形（户外台阶、室内结构）持续行走。

Conclusion: 围绕预期接触点组织视觉历史信息，可显著提升人形机器人感知和行走能力，有助于未来复杂环境下的自主行走机器人开发。

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion over complex terrain requires anticipating footholds that may no longer be visible at touchdown. Limited camera coverage and self-occlusion make it necessary to retrieve relevant terrain information from earlier observations. We present FootQuery, a perceptive locomotion framework that queries depth history using each foot's predicted next touchdown. The policy predicts touchdown locations and uncertainty from proprioception and uses these distributions, together with per-foot features, to query sparsely sampled historical depth frames. During training, realized contacts are projected into historical images to supervise retrieval at the regions where those contacts were visible. The retrieved per-foot features are fused with global visual memory to generate control actions. A progressive force-assistance curriculum supports early exploration, while event-consistent tread-midline shaping encourages coordinated stair contacts. Deployment requires only proprioception and onboard depth images. In simulation, the complete framework outperforms its component ablations on the most challenging tested stairs, gaps, and platforms. Real-world experiments on a Unitree G1 demonstrate continuous traversal with a single policy across outdoor stairs and indoor routes combining stair ascent and descent, platforms, and gaps. These results support organizing visual history around anticipated contacts for perceptive humanoid locomotion.

</details>


### [191] [Robotic Multiphase Interaction: Manipulating Coupled Liquid and Solid Dynamics with a World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21448)
*Yixuan Feng,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的机器人多相交互（RMI）研究范式，首次实现了机器人对液体与可变形多孔固体的耦合操控与状态预测，以湿润海绵为实例实现显著提升液体保留量预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人多操控固体物体或液体和固体保持分离，尚未系统研究流体渗入可变形多孔固体（如湿海绵）时的力学-流体耦合效应，而此类场景在家用和制造业中广泛存在，涉及抓取稳定性和安全性等实际问题。

Method: 采用不可压缩多孔流体的隐式建模（基于平滑粒子流体动力学），结合库仑接触记忆机制、速度力混合控制与抓举稳定性门限，实现机器人对湿润海绵动态操控。通过动作条件化世界模型预测固-液耦合状态变化，动作生成则采用U-Net架构与Diffusion Policy或流匹配方法。

Result: 所提世界模型较基线显著降低60%以上水分保留预测误差，基于世界模型遴选最优动作序列后末端水误差进一步减半，表明准确建模固-液耦合状态能有效提升机器人对复杂物理变化的预测和控制能力。

Conclusion: 本工作首次系统建模并验证了机器人在多相耦合环境中的操控潜能，对未来机器人在含湿多孔物体操作和安全应用方面具有重要推动意义。

Abstract: This work presents \textit{Robotic Multiphase Interaction (RMI)}, a setting in which liquid enters a porous material and interacts mechanically with its deforming solid skeleton. Manipulation can therefore change pore volume, expel or redistribute retained liquid, and alter grasp stability at the same time. Spilled liquid can also create safety risks in domestic and manufacturing settings. This differs from most manipulation of solid objects and from tasks that involve both liquid and solid while keeping the phases spatially separate. We study a sponge filled with water as the first RMI example. We use implicit incompressible porous flow with smoothed particle hydrodynamics as the dynamics engine and enable robotic manipulation by adding Coulomb contact memory, hybrid velocity and force regulation, and a stability gate for lifting. The resulting environment connects robot commands to changes in the coupled liquid and solid state. A world model conditioned on actions predicts how this state evolves under candidate commands, while a temporal UNet generates actions using either Diffusion Policy or rectified flow matching. Our world model reduces retained water prediction error by more than $60\%$ compared with the baseline. The best action sequence selected by the world model from policy proposals further reduces the predicted terminal water error by about half. These improvements show that modelling the coupled liquid and solid state helps the robot predict how its actions affect both the porous object and the liquid held inside.

</details>


### [192] [AtomEgo: Exploring Ego-Robot Integration for Embodied Foundation Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21461)
*Di Wu,Dongchen Zheng,Junhe Sheng,Zhongxing Wei,Songxin Zhang,Zejian Xie,Xiaoquan Sun,Junyang Zheng,Zhuoyang Song,Jiaxing Zhang,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过联合自我视角（egocentric human）与机器人数据进行共训练（co-training）的策略，旨在提高机器人基础模型的泛化能力，并验证了数据规模与对齐质量共同决定能力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人基础模型受到机器人演示样本数量有限及多样性不足的制约，因此希望引入人类自我视角交互的大规模数据。然而，由于人类与机器人在身体结构和动作空间存在较大差异，如何有效地将这种数据纳入模型预训练尚未明晰。

Method: 构建了AtomEgo框架，整理了约2659小时的自我视角数据，并开发了可扩展的数据处理流程。在视觉-语言-动作和世界-动作两类基础模型架构下，系统性比较了三种训练范式：1）带领域专用动作头的联合共训练；2）逐步的人-机对齐迁移；3）联合视频-动作建模。通过多任务真实机器人实验及跨身体模型的语言条件表示分析进行验证。

Result: 实验证明，联合使用大量对齐良好的人类自我视角数据可以提升机器人在泛化任务上的表现。进一步揭示出能力提升取决于数据规模和对齐质量的乘积关系（Data Scale * Alignment Quality --> Capability Gain）。

Conclusion: 自我视角数据若能高效对齐并合理利用，对于提升机器人基础模型的泛化能力有明显帮助。研究提出的原则可为未来大规模机器人模型的预训练提供实践指导。

Abstract: Embodied foundation models are constrained by the limited scale and diversity of robot demonstrations, motivating the use of large-scale egocentric human interaction data. However, how to effectively incorporate such data into embodied-model pre-training remains unclear because of substantial embodiment and action-space gaps between humans and robots. We present AtomEgo, a systematic study of ego--robot co-training supported by a curated corpus of approximately 2,659 hours and a scalable data processing pipeline. Across vision--language--action and world--action model architectures, we investigate three representative paradigms: joint co-training with domain-specific action heads, progressive ego-to-robot transfer through embodiment alignment, and joint video--action modeling. We evaluate these paradigms through multi-task real-robot experiments and language-conditioned cross-embodiment representation analysis. Our results reveal a simple principle: Data Scale * Alignment Quality --> Capability Gain; egocentric data can improve generalization, but their value depends on how effectively they are aligned and utilized. This principle can provide practical guidance for scalable ego--robot pre-training.

</details>


### [193] [Learning Distance-Conditioned Object Transport for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation from a Single Motion Clip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21467)
*Yuhyeon Hwang,Daniel Sungho Jung,YongHyeok Seo,Mingi Jung,Chang Nho Cho,Jung-Hoon Hwang,Dongin Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法DCRR，使仿人机器人不仅能够复现演示轨迹的最终结果，还能根据不同指令灵活完成不同距离的物体搬运。实验在多种任务和真实硬件上验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于运动跟踪复现的行为克隆方法，只重视演示结尾的终止状态，对于中间经过的状态缺乏泛化能力，导致机器人只能完成预定距离的物品搬运，缺乏根据目标距离自适应搬运能力。

Method: 提出Distance-Conditioned Reference Recomposition（DCRR）方法，将演示轨迹的终端段移到中间状态，并用“冻结”的追踪教师网络在闭环控制下复现，得到不同距离下的示范轨迹。最终利用行为克隆将经验蒸馏到无参考策略中。

Result: 在Carry、Kick-Push、Crouch-Push和Drag等多任务上，DCRR-BC方法明显优于只使用原始行为克隆，平均归一化距离MAE为0.15（对比源轨迹策略的0.28）；经RL微调后运动鲁棒性进一步提升。真实机器人实验也表明该方法可实现指令相关的多距离搬运。

Conclusion: DCRR方法显著提升了机器人搬运任务中对搬运距离的可控性及泛化能力，为基于演示的机器人操控提供了新的思路，并在仿真和真实硬件上均展示了有效性。

Abstract: Motion tracking can reproduce humanoid loco-manipulation from a single retargeted motion clip, but a policy trained on a fixed reference primarily reproduces its demonstrated transport outcome. Although the source trajectory visits intermediate object displacements, transport termination is demonstrated only at its endpoint. We identify this mismatch as the termination-versus-passage gap: intermediate displacements are observed as passage states rather than termination-complete outcomes. We introduce Distance-Conditioned Reference Recomposition (DCRR), which relocates the demonstrated termination segment to intermediate transport states. A frozen tracking teacher replays the recomposed references under closed-loop dynamics, and the retained trajectories are relabeled by their achieved object placements and distilled into a reference-free policy. This procedure constructs distance-conditioned supervision from the interaction behavior encoded in the source motion. Across Carry, Kick-Push, Crouch-Push, and Drag, DCRR-BC produces command-dependent transport with an overall normalized distance mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.15, compared with 0.28 for source-only behavior cloning. RL fine-tuning further improves the command response and execution robustness in the training simulator and under sim-to-sim transfer. Finally, hardware experiments demonstrate transport-distance modulation across all four interaction modes.

</details>


### [194] [Adaptive Rollout Truncation Based on Epistemic Uncertainty for Efficient Offline World Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21482)
*Nikodem Sebastian Zymla,Laurin Thiele,Johannes Pitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用认知不确定性自适应调整训练推理步数的新方法，在保证预测精度的同时大幅减少计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于模型的机器人学习中，精确的神经世界模型能够预测未来状态，但传统的多步自回归训练需要固定的推理步数，导致计算成本高且错误积累，未能根据模型的不确定性动态调整训练步数。

Method: 引入了一种基于认知（epistemic）不确定性的自适应推理截断方法。训练初期用两阶段预热以校准不确定性阈值，随后在训练过程中当模型的不确定性达到阈值时提前终止推理。文中分别尝试了基于五头集成和Monte Carlo Dropout的两种不确定性估计方法。

Result: 在ANYmal-D和ANT环境实验中，基于集成模型的不确定性自适应截断方法在保持甚至提升预测精度的同时，训练所需推理步数较固定步长和RWM-U基线大幅减少。例如在ANYmal-D上用本文方法的计算量约为基线的28%。

Conclusion: 认知不确定性能有效引导自适应推理长度，提升世界模型训练的计算效率，不仅有助于下游策略正则化，也有助于本身的高效训练。

Abstract: Accurate neural world models are central to model-based robotics, where they enable robots to predict future states from previously observed trajectories. Multi-step autoregressive training improves long-horizon prediction, but fixed rollout horizons also increase computational cost and can amplify early training errors when the model is still inaccurate. Existing training schemes typically use the same rollout length throughout optimization, independent of the model's current predictive reliability.
  We propose an epistemic uncertainty-driven adaptive rollout strategy for offline world model training following an auto-curriculum training scheme. Instead of always unrolling to a fixed horizon, the model terminates autoregressive rollouts once epistemic uncertainty exceeds a threshold calibrated from a warm-up phase. We study two uncertainty estimators: a five-head ensemble with a shared recurrent backbone and Monte Carlo Dropout. A two-stage warm-up procedure stabilizes uncertainty estimates before we enable adaptive truncation.
  Experiments on ANYmal-D and ANT show that ensemble-based adaptive truncation matches or improves the prediction accuracy of fixed-horizon training and the RWM-U baseline while requiring substantially fewer cumulative rollout steps. Training a world model on ANYmal-D following the presented approach reaches comparable final performance with the baselines with roughly 72% less rollout computation. These results indicate that epistemic uncertainty is useful not only for downstream policy regularization, but also for making world model training itself more compute-efficient.

</details>


### [195] [FORTE: Task-Adaptive Force Capability Optimization for Mobile Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21497)
*Xiao Wang,Heng Zhang,Gokhan Solak,Fei Zhao,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种面向任务的力能力优化框架，用于冗余移动操作臂，通过利用视觉-语言模型推理物体物理属性，实现实现力需求自适应与高操作性平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有冗余度解析方法在机械臂任务中，要么忽略具体任务的受力需求，要么一味最大化力能力，导致在任务对大力容量没需求时牺牲了操控性。因此需要方法根据任务个性化、不盲目地配置力输出，并兼顾操作灵活性。

Method: 1）用视觉-语言模型（VLM）结合RGB图像和任务描述推断目标物体物理属性；2）生成能反映重力与惯性需求的期望任务受力序列；3）定义任务导向力能力度量标准，并将其与RFP残余力多面体距离联系起来，量化任务需求和机器人剩余驱动力的匹配程度；4）把该度量与操作空间能力、关节限位避免、轨迹平滑性、底座振荡抑制等目标综合进全身运动轨迹多目标优化问题。

Result: 在不同负载条件下的举重与单点悬挂实验显示，提出的方法让机械臂在重负载时具备足够力能力，在轻负载时能维持高操作性，实现根据任务需要自适应分配力与灵活性的能力。这一特性超过了单纯最大化RFP容量或者只优先操控性的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在不同任务负载需求下，实现移动操作臂力输出与操作性的动态平衡，为多样实际应用带来更高效可靠的解决途径。实现代码已开源。

Abstract: Effective physical interaction control in robotic manipulation requires not only kinematically feasible motion but also sufficient force-interaction capability. Existing redundancy resolution methods often ignore task-specific force demands or maximize the force capability indiscriminately, sacrificing dexterity when large force margins are unnecessary. We propose a task-oriented force capability optimization framework for redundant mobile manipulators. A Vision-Language Model (VLM) infers object physical properties from an RGB image and a task description, generating a desired task-force sequence that captures gravitational and inertial demands. We then define a task-oriented force capability metric as the signed distance between a task-force uncertainty ball and the dynamic residual force polytope (RFP), quantifying compatibility between task demands and the robot's remaining actuation capacity. This metric is incorporated, alongside manipulability, joint-limit avoidance, trajectory smoothness, and base-oscillation suppression, into a whole-body multi-objective trajectory-optimization problem. Experiments on a mobile manipulator performing lifting and single-point-holding tasks under varying payload conditions demonstrate that the proposed method provides sufficient force capability for heavy loads while preserving high manipulability for light loads. This yields a task-adaptive balance that fixed capability-maximizing baselines (RFP inscribed radius, RFP cone) and manipulability-only optimization fail to achieve. The core implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/yeying256/FORTE.

</details>


### [196] [DPed-VLN: A Benchmark for Socially Compliant Vision-and-Language Navigation in Dynamic Pedestrian Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21504)
*Haojie Dai,Xiangyi Wang,Liuyi Wang,Kai Sheng,Zongtao He,Chengju Liu,Wei Ye,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新型的动态行人视觉语言导航基准（DPed-VLN），并引入了感知行人的导航策略网络（DPet）。对现有SOTA模型通过LoRA进行适配并实验，展现了显著提升导航效率与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往视觉-语言导航任务多聚焦于静态室内环境，实际机器人应用要求能应对动态行人和社会安全约束，因此亟需针对动态人群场景设计任务与基准。

Method: 1）提出DPed-VLN基准，包含多场景、多类型任务说明及社会约束路径。
2）引入由ORCA控制的仿真动态行人。
3）提出DPet网络，通过强化学习与模仿学习联合训练感知行人。
4）选取代表性SOTA模型（如NaVILA、StreamVLN），使用LoRA微调适配新任务。

Result: 1）LoRA微调提升多个原始基线（VLM模型）的成功率与安全指标表现，显著降低如StreamVLN的碰撞率；2）DPet-RL方法在成功率(SR)、路径长度分数(SPL)、社会安全分(STL)等主要指标均取得最佳表现。

Conclusion: 引入的DPed-VLN为动态人群场景下的导航研究奠定了新基准，DPet模型和LoRA适配显著提升了机器人应对动态社会约束的能力，对实际应用具有积极意义。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) has advanced rapidly in static indoor environments, but robots operating in human-populated spaces must ground language while responding to moving pedestrians and social-safety constraints. We present DPed-VLN, a Habitat 3.0 benchmark for dynamic-pedestrian VLN that couples 33,093 navigation episodes with paired global and prior-augmented instructions, ORCA-controlled humanoid pedestrians, socially constrained expert paths, and metrics that jointly assess navigation efficiency and social safety. DPed-VLN separates ordinary goal-oriented route guidance from prior-augmented instructions that expose dynamic-pedestrian cues for controlled analysis. To instantiate the benchmark, we introduce DPet (Dynamic Pedestrian-aware Network), a pedestrian-aware policy network trained with reinforcement learning and imitation learning. We further adapt representative state-of-the-art VLM-based navigation models, including NaVILA and StreamVLN, to DPed-VLN through LoRA fine-tuning. Experiments show that LoRA adaptation improves zero-shot VLM baselines in several success and safety metrics, especially reducing StreamVLN's collision rate. Among the evaluated methods, DPet-RL achieves the highest SR, SPL, and STL.

</details>


### [197] [2nd Place Solution to the HANDS 2026 Workshop Challenge-Dexterous Grasp Motion Track: Single-Shot Trajectory Warping for Grasp Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21511)
*Muneeb A. Khan,Woojin Kim,Shinwoo Kim,Muhammad Munsif,Binod Bhattarai,Seungryul Baek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的灵巧手抓取运动生成方法，通过对已有成功演示进行整体变形，而非逐步生成动作，实现了高难度抓取任务的高成功率，在比赛中获得第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dexterous Grasp Motion任务由于手具有高自由度，每步决策空间庞大且误差易积累，加上训练与测试对象和物理环境的变化，使得生成稳定的抓取轨迹具有挑战性。作者希望找到更高效、鲁棒的抓取运动生成方案。

Method: 作者提出直接对一条成功的GraspM3的示范进行整体编辑，使用策略网络一次性感知目标后输出一个12维的变形量，并在仿真里重放变形过的动作。训练时，对4824个训练对象并行用一步PPO方式更新变形策略。

Result: 该方法在比赛easy track的测试集上达到了94.61%的成功率（最高），hard track上为57.18%，表现优异。

Conclusion: 利用对演示动作的整体变形和高效训练策略可以有效减少决策维度和误差积累，极大提升仿真灵巧手抓取任务的成功率，方案在实际挑战中表现突出。

Abstract: This report describes our 2nd place solution to the HANDS 2026 workshop challenge (Dexterous Grasp Motion track) in conjunction with ECCV 2026. In this challenge, we address grasp motion generation for the 12-DoF LinkerHand O6, aiming to produce physically plausible reach-and-lift trajectories for unseen objects from randomized initial hand poses in simulation. This task is particularly challenging because each grasp requires a per-step policy to make approximately $70$ twelve-dimensional decisions, with errors accumulating over time, while test objects and physical dynamics may differ from those encountered during training. To address these challenges, we propose editing a single successful GraspM3 demonstration instead of generating the motion step by step: a policy observes the object once and outputs a 12-D warp of the demonstration, which is then replayed open-loop. Moreover, we train the warp policy with one-step PPO over all $4{,}824$ training objects in parallel. As a result, our method achieved success rates of $94.61\%$ on the easy track, the highest of all submissions, and $57.18\%$ on the hard track of the private test set.

</details>


### [198] [Skel-WAM: A Hand-Skeleton-Conditioned World Action Model for Human-to-Robot Manipulation Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21514)
*Zetao Cai,Yaping Li,Yiqun Wang,Xinyu Zhan,Yuyin Yang,Haoxiang Ma,Kailin Li,Tao Lu,Jiangmiao Pang,Linning Xu,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的方法Skel-WAM，通过统一的人手-骨骼运动接口，使机器人能够高效利用人类演示视频进行学习，显著提升了机器人多任务的成功率和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示数据采集成本高，覆盖任务变异有限；而人类视频数据获取便宜但因人机之间的外观和动作空间差异难以直接用于机器人学习。

Method: Skel-WAM提出采用统一的手部骨骼拓扑结构，将人类和机器人的动作通过骨骼叠加和结构化的2.5维关键点进行对齐。系统使用视觉专家与关键点专家通过混合Transformers共同学习动态，再由单独的机器人训练专家将预测映射为可执行动作。分离的结构支持用人类视频监督共享动态，无需对人类视频进行机器人动作标注。

Result: 在4项真实双手操作任务和7项模拟任务中，Skel-WAM的平均成功率分别为79.86%和63.29%，比最强基线高出22.22和8.28个百分点。对于未在机器人演示中出现的任务变体，采用人机联合训练技术后的成功率提升了两倍多（38.89%到86.11%）。

Conclusion: 通过骨骼接口实现了人类与机器人数据的联合学习，大大拓展了机器人任务覆盖面，人类演示为机器人学习提供了有效补充。

Abstract: Robot demonstrations are expensive to collect and often provide limited distributional coverage of task variations. Human videos offer a low-cost source of complementary manipulation experience, but learning from them requires bridging embodiment gaps in visual appearance and action spaces. We introduce Skel-WAM, a world action model that bridges these differences through a unified hand-skeleton motion interface. The key insight is to align human and robot motion through a common hand topology, combining skeleton overlays that ground motion in the scene with structured 2.5-D keypoints that encode explicit hand kinematics. Video and Keypoint Experts jointly learn visual and skeletal dynamics through a Mixture-of-Transformers, while a separate robot-trained Action Expert maps these predictions to executable controls. This separation enables human and robot demonstrations to directly supervise shared dynamics without requiring robot action labels for human videos. Across four real-world bimanual tasks and seven simulated tasks, Skel-WAM achieves average success rates of 79.86% and 63.29%, surpassing the strongest baseline by 22.22 and 8.28 percentage points, respectively. Human-robot cotraining more than doubles real-world success on task variations absent from robot training data, from 38.89% to 86.11%. These results demonstrate that a shared skeletal interface enables joint learning across human and robot data and expands robot task coverage through complementary human demonstrations.

</details>


### [199] [SABER: Learning Attention-based Semantic Affordance for Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21572)
*Hari Prasanth Palanivelu,Samuel Sze,Kennard Garrison Johannes,Albertus Hendrawan Adiwahono,Meng Yee,Chuah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SABER，一种结合地形几何与语义接触权限、无需规划器的强化学习步态策略，并实现在真实四足机器人上的语义避障行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人虽然能够根据地形几何选择落足点，但往往忽略了地形的语义信息，如易碎物品或不可接触区域，导致安全隐患或设备受损。为提升机器人在工业环境中的部署安全性，必须融合地形语义信息。

Method: 提出无规划器的强化学习策略SABER。将地形的3D几何与语义接触成本映射为统一的地形可通行性图（terrain-affordance map），通过在cross-attention机制中加入由接触成本门控的语义偏置，不同距离的禁止区域会动态调整注意力分布，从而指导步态避开不可接触区域。

Result: 通过消融实验显示，移除语义偏置会导致被禁止区域的接触率上升55%，而速度跟踪能力基本不变。在实际Unitree B2机器人上进行了实际与仿真验证，能够在多类语义障碍物下精准选择落足点。

Conclusion: SABER策略能有效结合地形几何与语义信息，实现工业环境下更安全、更可靠的四足机器人行走，并已在真实场景验证语义避障能力。

Abstract: Perceptive legged locomotion has advanced rapidly by integrating terrain geometry into learned policies, yet the integration of terrain meaning remains sparse: a pipe, a patch of grass, or a fragile box may be geometrically traversable while being inappropriate for contact. In industrial environments, where legged robots increasingly operate, a single misplaced step can damage fragile equipment, destabilize the robot, or endanger the site. To address this, we introduce SABER, a planner-free reinforcement-learning policy that jointly reasons about terrain geometry and semantic contact permission. The policy consumes a unified terrain-affordance map, where each cell encodes local 3D geometry and a semantic contact cost. We augment cross-attention with a learned, signed semantic bias: an additive term on the attention logits, gated by the contact cost, that reweights flagged cells by their distance from the nearest foot. A hazard therefore reshapes attention where it can still affect the next foothold, and its influence fades where it cannot. The resulting policy selects footholds on permitted support and keeps the leg clear of forbidden regions throughout the swing phase. We perform a systematic ablation that isolates the contribution of each architectural component; removing the semantic bias alone increases forbidden contacts by 55% while velocity tracking is unchanged. We validate the policy on a Unitree B2, demonstrating sim-to-real semantic contact selection across indoor and outdoor environments and four semantic obstacle classes.

</details>


### [200] [Tilt as a Certified Resource: Preserving Motor Wrench-Rate Authority on Articulated Multirotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21580)
*Giuseppe Silano,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种面向多旋翼无人机的主动形态分配方法，通过控制伺服倾斜及电机出力，实现更高效、更安全的飞行姿态与外力应对能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分配器主要关注努力最小化，但忽略了动力学极限，忽略了在扰动下迅速调节能力的需求。直接最大化“机动储备”会导致解空间不连通，且对于固定几何结构出现权衡死结：电机调整无法在不损失跟踪精度的情况下提升可控能力。解决该零和悖论是本文的动力。

Method: 引入主动形态（如伺服倾斜）作为几何资源，通过构建依赖飞行器实时构型、电机独立的“动力准备证书”（以可达外力速率集合的对数体积刻画），杜绝伺服机械极限的虚假冗余。证书作为控制障碍函数（CBF）嵌入统一物理命令二次规划方案，对电机与伺服同时分配命令。

Result: 在剧烈阵风下的闭环仿真表明，传统分配方式会发散，未认证的主动形态分配会突破安全极限，而CBF过滤器能够有效约束系统状态，维持飞行器的机动储备。

Conclusion: 通过主动形态与动力准备证书结合控制障碍函数方法，可在强扰动下保障多旋翼平台的安全并提升生存能力，突破了单纯依赖电机的零和困境。

Abstract: Fully-actuated multirotor aerial vehicles must not only track nominal wrenches but retain the "readiness" to modulate them rapidly under disturbances. Classical effort-minimizing allocators ignore this dynamic limit, whereas maximizing readiness leads to topologically disconnected optimal sheets demanding physically impossible actuator rates. Enforcing a readiness safety floor on fixed-geometry symmetric platforms further encounters a zero-sum degeneracy: motor-speed redistribution cannot improve authority without conceding wrench tracking. This paper uses active morphology to break the degeneracy, treating servo tilt as a geometric resource supplying authority-recovery directions unavailable to static rotors. We construct a configuration-dependent, motor-only readiness certificate - the log-volume of the reachable wrench-rate set - that explicitly excludes servo capacity, preventing a "ghost capacity fallacy" in which the certificate would falsely credit slow mechanical kinematic limits instead of collapsing accurately at motor saturation. The certificate is enforced as a Control Barrier Function (CBF) within a Unified Physical-Command Quadratic Program acting on motor torques and servo setpoints. Closed-loop simulations of an articulated octorotor under severe gust disturbances show classical allocators diverging and uncertified articulated allocators violating the safety floor, while the proposed CBF filter bounds the system state and preserves vehicle authority.

</details>


### [201] [A High-Payload Wall-Climbing Robot Using Passive Bistable Suction Cups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21584)
*Andrew Nguyen,Mingyuan Li,Daniel Bruder*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种利用双稳态无源吸盘的新型高载重壁面爬行机器人，能够在不同平滑表面上自动行走并携带重物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统壁面爬行机器人采用有源吸附，虽能承载较重物体，但需要持续供电维持吸附；无源吸附如传统吸盘虽不耗能但载重有限。为兼顾能耗与载重，需新的吸附方式。

Method: 本文采用创新设计的双稳态无源吸盘，无需机器人主动向墙体施压即实现吸附，并通过履带式运动系统自动控制吸盘的吸附与释放，实现高效壁面行走。

Result: 该机器人可实现在玻璃、木材、金属及涂漆等平滑表面上的垂直、侧壁及倒挂行走，最大可拖拽7.94公斤（载重与自重比达2.25）的重物。

Conclusion: 利用双稳态无源吸盘的壁面爬行机器人兼具高负载和低能耗优势，拓展了其在危险或高劳动密集场景如清洁、检测和维护等自动化应用的潜力。

Abstract: Wall-climbing robots capable of scaling vertical surfaces could help automate hazardous or labor intensive tasks such as window washing, inspection, maintenance, and construction. Active adhesion methods achieve higher payload capacities, but require power to maintain their grip. Passive adhesion devices such as suction cups are an attractive option for such robots because they do not require power to maintain their grip, but they are limited by their payload capacity. This work presents a novel high-payload wall-climbing robot that utilizes passive bistable suction cups to generate adhesion without needing to be pushed into the wall. The robot features a track-based system that automatically engages and disengages bistable suction cups to achieve locomotion on smooth surfaces. The robot is able to achieve vertical wall climbing on glass, wood, metal, and painted surfaces, sideways and upside-down climbing, and is able to tow a payload of 7.940 kg (with a payload-to-weight ratio of 2.25).

</details>


### [202] [Potential-Field Action Representation for Reinforcement Learning in Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21609)
*Xinyu Liu,Gökhan Solak,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于人工势场（PA-RL）的强化学习方法，用于提升机器人操作任务中的动作表示，有效提高任务成功率并优化运动品质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于强化学习的机器人操作中，现有的方法通常需要策略同时学会高层次任务与底层运动控制，再加上直接输出运动指令的动作空间，使训练难度大，效率低，且物理执行表现受限。如何设计更有效的动作表示，以简化学习并提升性能，是当前研究的难点。

Method: 提出在RL中用人工势场的参数作为动作输出，让策略调节势场参数，由势场自动生成指引方向，再通过笛卡尔阻抗控制器执行，实现低级控制与高级策略分离。方法在peg-in-hole插入等具代表性的接触丰富任务上进行了仿真与真实机器人实验，并与其他多种动作空间进行了对比。

Result: 在仿真插入任务中，PA-RL唯一能够在规定训练时间内达到100%的成功率，最佳对比基线为92.6%。此外，PA-RL显著减少了关节力矩变化（55.4%）和笛卡尔加速度变化（70.8%），并且没有额外的运动质量奖励项。迁移到真实机器人时，PA-RL无需微调即可9/9成功完成插入任务。

Conclusion: PA-RL通过引入人工势场作为动作空间，有效简化了政策学习，提高了机器人的操作鲁棒性与运动质量，对接触丰富型任务具有较强的通用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Model-free reinforcement learning can acquire contact-rich robotic manipulation skills through trial-and-error interaction, but it often requires the policy to learn both task strategy and low-level motion generation. In this setting, the action representation is critical because it determines how policy outputs are converted into robot motion, shaping both exploration and physical execution. Direct Cartesian command interfaces require the policy to generate motion at every decision step, coupling task-level adaptation with continuous low-level control and increasing the learning burden. We propose PA-RL, a reinforcement-learning framework that uses artificial potential fields as the action representation. Instead of commanding motion directly, the policy adapts the parameters of an energy-like potential field, which generates a state-dependent guidance direction executed through a Cartesian impedance controller. We evaluate PA-RL on peg-in-hole insertion, a representative contact-rich task with nonlinear dynamics and discontinuous contact transitions. In simulation, PA-RL is compared with Cartesian velocity, Cartesian pose, and variable-impedance action spaces using the same RL algorithm. PA-RL is the only method to reach a 100% evaluation success rate within the allotted training time, while the best baseline reaches 92.6%. It also reduces joint-torque variation by 55.4% and Cartesian acceleration variation by 70.8% relative to the best baseline, without explicit motion-quality penalties in the reward. The simulation-trained policy further completes 9/9 real-robot insertions without fine-tuning, demonstrating the deployment feasibility of the learned potential-field interface.

</details>


### [203] [CounterPlay: Counterfactual Post-Training for Self-Play Driving Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21617)
*Jiarong Wei,Yin Wu,Runkai He,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CounterPlay的反事实自对弈后训练方法，通过在失败任务点回溯并以不同驾驶风格重新尝试，提高自动驾驶策略在模拟环境下的泛化能力和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自对弈在高吞吐量模拟器中虽然能训练出稳健的驾驶策略，但越到后期训练带来的收益越小，失败场景反复出现且难以解决。现有后训练方法局限于在失败状态点进行局部改进，难以从更早阶段改变驾驶风格避免同样的失败。

Method: CounterPlay包括三大核心组件：1）基于失败检测的回溯机制，利用策略的价值估计选择更早的存储状态重新尝试任务；2）奖励条件化机制，使单一策略能够在重试中用不同的驾驶风格（如保守到激进）尝试；3）仅保留未带来新增或更早碰撞/驶离的成功重试，并在部署条件下将这些策略提炼到最终策略中。

Result: 实验在BehaviorBench数据集上，CounterPlay在Interactive和Random分割、八类交通场景下均取得了SOTA表现，1B的后训练步数达到了仅用1%普通自对弈预算（100B）下的显著提升。其提升在不同驾驶风格下均成立，极大地减少了超时失败案例，实现了任务完成率和安全性的均衡。

Conclusion: CounterPlay突破了传统自对弈和后训练方法的局限，通过反事实回溯和奖励条件化，大幅提升了自动驾驶策略在复杂场景下的鲁棒性和实用性，是后训练领域的重要进展。

Abstract: Self-play in high-throughput simulators yields driving policies with robust closed-loop performance, but improvement per unit of simulation diminishes as training scales. Policies learn to handle common situations early, while further rollouts repeatedly encounter unresolved failures. Post-training offers an opportunity to target these failures, but existing methods primarily evaluate alternative actions or continuations at visited states, although successful recovery may require changing driving style earlier. We propose CounterPlay, a counterfactual self-play post-training approach that backtracks from failed tasks and retries them under alternate driving styles. CounterPlay rests on three key components. First, failure-driven backtracking uses the policy's value estimates to select an earlier stored state from which to retry the task. Second, reward conditioning enables a single policy to retry the task from this state using candidate styles ranging from cautious to aggressive. Third, CounterPlay retains task-completing retries only if no other vehicle incurs a new or earlier collision or off-road event relative to the factual branch. Retries that pass verification with fresh randomness are then distilled into the policy under its deployment condition. On BehaviorBench, CounterPlay achieves state-of-the-art scores on both the Interactive and Random splits across all eight traffic regimes using 1B post-training transitions, which is just 1% of the anchor's 100B self-play training budget. Improvements over the anchor hold across all three evaluated driving styles. CounterPlay resolves a substantial fraction of the anchor's timeout cases on BehaviorBench and achieves a balance between task completion and safety that neither continued self-play nor adopting a more aggressive driving style attains.

</details>


### [204] [Towards Fine-Grained Object Manipulation: SAM3-Guided Visuomotor Policy with Persistent Memory Learning and Focused Visual Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21621)
*Haolong Meng,Fangbo Qin,Mengchen Bai,Houwu Wang,Cirong Liu,Shan Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种针对细粒度物体操作任务的视觉运动智能框架，提升了机器人区分和操作外观相似物体的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉引导方法难以区分同类别的细粒度物体，且在有干扰物情况下，机器人操作的准确性和可靠性较差。

Method: 提出FOM-SAM3模块，通过有限多视角配准图像学习出可复用的物体记忆向量，并使用Focus Spatial-Appearance Encoding (FSAE) 将物体的外观特征与位置编码结合，指导行动策略（如扩散策略和Transformer分块动作方法）。

Result: 在FO-30数据集及3个真实机器人细粒度物体操作任务上，所提方法可在场景干扰下精准区分相似物体，表现出良好的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FOM-SAM3框架显著提升了机器人在复杂场景下细粒度物体区分与操作的能力，为未来相关研究和应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Fine-grained object (FO) manipulation requires robots to distinguish a specified FO from visually similar objects and execute actions reliably despite scene distractors. However, scene-level visual conditioning lacks explicit object selection, while category-level guidance cannot reliably distinguish FOs within the same category. We present a SAM3-guided visuomotor framework that addresses these challenges through persistent object memory and focused visual conditioning. First, we introduce FO Memory-driven SAM3 (FOM-SAM3), which learns reusable FO memory tokens from limited multi-view registration images while keeping SAM3 fully frozen. Through one-vs-rest learning, these tokens encode persistent memories for localizing target FOs and rejecting similar alternatives, which can be stored in a memory bank. Second, we propose Focused Spatial-Appearance Encoding (FSAE), which combines in-FO local appearance features with explicit bounding-box coordinates to condition action policies including Diffusion Policy (DP) and Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT). The effectiveness of the proposed FOM-SAM3 was validated on the FO-30 dataset comprising 30 physical objects across four coarse categories. Across three real-robot FO manipulation tasks, our FOM-SAM3-guided policies demonstrated robustness against distractors, discrimination ability among similar FOs, and extendibility to new FOs.

</details>


### [205] [SynthDemo-RL: Breaking the Zero-Reward Barrier in VLA Adaptation with LLM-Guided Synthetic Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21650)
*Hiroaki Kingetsu,Hiroaki Kurihara,Kaoru Yokoo,Kenji Fukumizu,Manohar Kaul*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了SynthDemo-RL方法，通过合成演示与强化学习结合，显著提升了视觉-语言-动作模型在无人工示范任务中的表现，尤其是在稀疏奖励场景下提升了探索能力和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型微调高度依赖人工遥操作演示，强化学习在二元稀疏奖励下面临极度探索困难，特别是在缺乏成功演示的任务（如LIBERO-PRO）中，政策难以获得有效提升。

Method: （1）由“教师”自动将模拟环境中可获取的特权状态信息转化为成功操作演示轨迹；（2）通过监督微调（SFT）将这些轨迹蒸馏给学生VLA模型；（3）利用PPO和任务成功二元奖励进一步精炼学生模型。训练主要无须新增人工演示。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO基准的57个任务中，原始微调模型有27项任务成功率为0%。直接PPO微调可使10项任务复苏，有17项仍为0%；而SynthDemo-RL能全部复苏这27项，成功率达97%以上。在标准LIBERO集上，无人工演示情况下，平均成功率达96%，接近用50个人工演示训练的模型。

Conclusion: SynthDemo-RL方法显著减轻了对人工演示的依赖，在稀疏奖励任务中大幅提升了VLA模型的探索能力与通用性，为实际机器人任务提供了更高效的数据利用与策略迁移路径。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models commonly relies on human teleoperation demonstrations, while reinforcement learning (RL) with sparse binary rewards faces an exploration challenge when successful trajectories are rarely sampled. We propose SynthDemo-RL, a teacher-student framework in which an automated teacher converts simulator-privileged state into successful manipulation trajectories, a VLA student is distilled from them by supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and PPO with binary task-success rewards refines the student. We study reward coverage, the fraction of tasks for which at least one success is observed under the fixed evaluation protocol, as a complement to the average success rate. On LIBERO-PRO, a public benchmark of perturbed LIBERO tasks for which no demonstrations exist, 27 of 57 scored tasks are at exactly 0% success for a pi_0.5 policy fine-tuned on the original LIBERO tasks. Direct PPO from this policy, under the same PPO recipe and the same RL compute as SynthDemo-RL's refinement stage, rescues 10 of these 27 tasks and leaves 17 at 0%. SynthDemo-RL, with 50 synthesized trajectories per task and no new human demonstrations, rescues all 27 and reaches average success rates of 97.8% and 97.1% on the Position and Task axes of LIBERO-PRO, respectively. On standard LIBERO, the same pipeline reaches 96.0% with no human demonstrations, within 1.7 points of pi_0.5 trained on 50 human demonstrations per task. We further validate the pipeline on RoboTwin 2.0 and verify that trajectories from a policy trained in a MuJoCo twin execute open-loop on a physical robot.

</details>


### [206] [Outcome-Conditioned End-Effector Geometry Across Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21659)
*Xingyu Lin,Zhuang Li,Zhongrun Wu,Shouquan Zhou,Dehui Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统地比较了不同视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，通过相同任务但不同动作接口执行时末端执行器的几何行为是否一致，以及量化了各种情况下它们的相似性和不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管不同的VLA策略可以成功完成相同的操作任务，但任务成功本身并不能说明它们在物理执行上的一致性，因此亟需评估它们在细粒度行为层面的协同与差异性。

Method: 作者基于15,000个LIBERO数据集中的闭环操作轨迹，分析了四种策略在相同初始条件下完成任务时的末端执行器轨迹距离，主要采用归一化动态时间规整距离（DTW）比较，不同成功/失败组合下统计策略间几何一致性。

Result: 当两个策略均成功时，轨迹距离显著低于仅有一个策略成功的情形（0.0120 m vs. 0.0380 m），且这一趋势在所有任务、策略对以及不同采样和频带表示下均成立。失败策略对距离更大但数据稀疏。成功轨迹间的差异比同任务演示间相近，且表现出策略间的残余差异。多种控制窗口和视觉压力下趋势仍然保持但量级变化。

Conclusion: VLA策略的物理执行在任务成功时更为接近，但低轨迹距离并不代表策略可直接互换。任务执行几何主要由任务属性影响，微观上策略间仍存在差异。不同评估窗口、视觉干扰等具体条件会影响策略间一致性的程度，但整体方向不变。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies solve the same manipulation task through different action interfaces, but task success alone does not establish whether their physical executions agree. We study cross-policy end-effector geometry in 15,000 closed-loop LIBERO rollouts from four policies. The primary clean-condition analysis forms 3,600 configuration-matched, and therefore dependent, policy pairs. Both-success pairs have a median normalized dynamic time warping distance of 0.0120 m versus 0.0380 m when exactly one policy succeeds. This ordering holds in every task, every policy pair, and nine sampling and band-limited representations; however, the ratio varies severalfold across representations, so we report the direction rather than a fixed multiple. Both-failure pairs are more separated again but rest on thin, uneven support, so we report them as exploratory. Within successful executions, partner replacements separate more across tasks than across initial states. A matched baseline still reveals measurable, heterogeneous residual policy differences, so a low cross-policy distance does not imply interchangeability. Successful executions sit about as far from same-task demonstrations as those demonstrations sit from each other, compatible with task-associated geometry without separating training-data overlap from task constraints. A common 72-action window preserves the ordering but reduces its magnitude; endpoint and duration adjustment likewise leaves a positive mixed-outcome coefficient relative to both-success pairs, though its magnitude is specification-dependent. Under composite visual stress, policy rankings and pair composition change together.

</details>


### [207] [RAYA: Learning Where and When to Intervene for Robot Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21690)
*Ishaan Mahajan,Charles Chen,Frederike Dümbgen,Brian Plancher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的机器人安全控制框架RAYA，能够主动调整任务和控制权，显著提升了在各类突发失效下的生存率。其方法在模拟和实物飞行实验中均显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人即便能预测即将发生的失效，往往由于反应过晚或原有任务权重不变而仍然无法及时恢复，因此急需一种能在决策过程中主动考虑恢复能力并动态调整任务优先级的控制方法。

Method: 提出RAYA框架：在优化控制器中嵌入由机器学习得到的有限时域恢复裕度（recoverability margin），并配合一个同样学得的、受限的调度器，能动态调整任务权重以最大化恢复可能性；方法在多种模拟环境与真实飞行器上进行了广泛实验。

Result: 在7200组无人机和自动驾驶仿真测试中，RAYA提升了机器人在不同扰动、轨迹、环境变化下的存活率，同时学得的组件无需额外训练即可迁移到新场景。在真实Crazyflie无人机上，在风和电机损失等极端情况下，RAYA全面优于3个现有基线，所有基线全数失效，而RAYA圆满完成全部任务。

Conclusion: RAYA能够实现比现有方法更具韧性的任务调整和恢复操作，显著提升了机器人在未知和极端失效场景下的鲁棒性和适应性，具有较强的实际工程意义。

Abstract: A robot can predict failure and still be unable to prevent it. By the time a safety mechanism reacts, the nominal plan may already have spent the control authority that recovery requires, and fixed task priorities may block whatever response remains. Our key insight is that both aspects are decided inside the controller. Recoverability must inform actions while they are chosen rather than veto them afterward, and task objectives must be adapted as recoverability shrinks. Building on this, we present RAYA, a hybrid learned-analytic framework that places a learned finite-horizon recoverability margin inside an optimal controller with hard constraints and pairs it with a bounded learned scheduler that shifts task weights to facilitate recovery. Across 7,200 simulation episodes per controller spanning quadrotor and autonomous-vehicle benchmarks, RAYA not only improves survival rates, but also transfers the learned components zero-shot to unseen trajectories, disturbances, plant shifts, and friction layouts. We developed an embedded realization of RAYA and deployed it on-board a 35g Crazyflie quadrotor. Across 40 combined hardware flights under wind with either aerodynamic mismatch or an unmodeled 40% motor-command loss, each of three baselines fails in all trials, while RAYA completes 10/10 six-cycle missions. Project Website: https://raya-control.github.io/.

</details>


### [208] [NeuRIO: A Streaming Neural Estimator for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Multi-Robot Relative Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21707)
*Zhehan Li,Jiadong Lu,Shengwei Ren,Chao Xu,Yanjun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NeuRIO是一种用于无锚点6自由度惯性测程的神经网络估算器，只需机器人间的测距、方向和IMU数据，具有很强的通用性和计算效率，支持大规模机器人团队并具备零样本仿真转实能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人惯性测程方法在团队规模、观测随时间变化和从仿真到实地的泛化方面存在局限，且传统优化方法在大规模机器人系统下的计算代价高昂。为了解决这些挑战，本文提出利用神经网络提升多机器人定位的可扩展性与鲁棒性。

Method: NeuRIO将传感器数据正则化到与重力对齐的坐标系，采用图神经网络结构表示机器人及其观测关系，利用注意力机制处理空间信息，通过GRU处理时序信息。整体网络结构支持不同规模与动态拓扑，训练时模拟多种运动、设备特性和持久传感器噪声，增强了泛化能力。

Result: 在24组真实场景实验中，NeuRIO实现了14.1厘米的位置RMSE和3.9度的旋转RMSE。仿真中能支持多达400个机器人时每次更新耗时低于20ms，而传统优化方法在24个机器人时已超过20ms。

Conclusion: NeuRIO不仅在精度和效率上优于传统方法，还能适应不同规模和动态变化的机器人团队，且支持零样本从仿真转为真实系统，展现了良好的工程应用前景。

Abstract: We present NeuRIO, a streaming neural estimator for anchor-free 6-DoF relative inertial odometry using only identified inter-robot bearings, ranges, and IMU measurements. NeuRIO canonicalizes measurements into gravity-aligned coordinates, represents robots as nodes and mutual observations as factors, and uses attention for spatial reasoning and GRUs for temporal modeling. As a graph network, NeuRIO applies shared node-wise and factor-wise operators throughout the network, enabling it to handle different team sizes and time-varying observation graphs. NeuRIO is trained on a simulator that couples various motion patterns, device-level sensor characteristics, and diverse, realistic modeled, and temporally persistent sensor corruptions. In this way, NeuRIO achieves zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. Across $24$ real-world sequences, NeuRIO achieves $14.1\,\mathrm{cm}$ position RMSE and $3.9^\circ$ rotation RMSE. More importantly, NeuRIO demonstrates strong computational scalability, maintaining an update cost below $20\,\mathrm{ms}$ with up to $400$ robots in simulation, while optimization-based methods exceed $20\,\mathrm{ms}$ at only $24$ robots. Moreover, even trained on limited team sizes, NeuRIO transfers directly to unseen larger teams without architectural or parameter changes.

</details>


### [209] [AgenticSwarm: Semantic Perception and Adaptive Task Allocation for Heterogeneous Multi-UAV Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21716)
*Muhammad Ahsan Mustafa,Yasheerah Yaqoot,Faryal Batool,Roohan Ahmed Khan,Valerii Serpiva,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了AgenticSwarm框架，用于多无人机在复杂环境下的语义感知与自适应任务分配，能根据环境变化和指令动态更新任务规划，提高多无人机系统的智能化和任务执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂环境下的多无人机任务需要理解环境语义和操作人员意图，同时能应对无人机故障、电量变化等动态条件，现有方法难以在感知、任务分配与动态适应之间实现紧密结合，因此亟需一种智能化自适应方案。

Method: 提出了AgenticSwarm框架，通过智能体解释航拍图像和自然语言指令，构建任务需求与环境的语义关联，融合约束任务分配（涵盖路径可行性、能耗、返航等），并能在任务执行中根据状态变化进行残余任务重构和连续自适应调整。

Result: 在五个不同模拟与一个室内真实环境中验证，AgenticSwarm针对语义感知和任务分配表现优越（相比基线方法，召回率提升25.2%，标签准确率提升29.5%），取消残余任务重构则导致重做任务激增和恢复效率剧降，说明其自适应性显著提升任务智能与效率。

Conclusion: AgenticSwarm能有效将语义推理与约束任务分配结合，具备强自适应能力，提升多无人机系统在复杂动态环境下的智能化任务执行效果。

Abstract: Multi UAV missions in complex environments require the system to understand both the surrounding scene and the intent of a human operator while maintaining feasible task allocation as mission conditions change. This paper presents AgenticSwarm, an agentic framework for semantic perception and adaptive task allocation in heterogeneous multi UAV missions. An agent interprets aerial imagery and natural language instructions to construct a grounded mission representation that links perceived objects and regions with task requirements, capability constraints, and mission dependencies. This information augments a constrained task allocation process in which obstacle aware path feasibility, energy consumption, and protected return home requirements are incorporated before assignment. During execution, changes such as UAV failure, battery degradation, or task modification trigger residual mission reconstruction from the current system state, while completed work and reconnaissance progress are retained. AgenticSwarm is evaluated across five diverse Gazebo environments and an indoor real test environment, demonstrating its ability to connect semantic reasoning with constrained allocation and adaptive multi UAV mission execution. Compared with a Grounding DINO+SAM~2.1 perception baseline, the SAM3-based pipeline improves class-aware recall by 25.2 percentage points (pp) and semantic label accuracy by 29.5 pp. Ablating residual mission replanning increases mean repeated work from 0% to 61.7% and post-event recovery time by 58.6%, highlighting the contribution of adaptive replanning to mission execution.

</details>


### [210] [A Novel Path-Tracking Algorithm for Automated Tractor-Trailer Forward and Backward Maneuvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21718)
*Alexandre Lombard,Florent Perronnet,Nicolas Gaud,Abdeljalil Abbas-Turki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的针对带有单挂车铰接车辆的路径跟踪算法，实现了无需单车校准或训练的精确稳定路径跟踪，可以胜过或媲美现有经典与先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物流、农业和工业领域，全自动拖挂车系统的路径跟踪对精度和鲁棒性要求极高。而拖车与牵引车之间的铰接结构导致非线性增强，尤其在倒车时大大增加了横纵向控制难度。因此需要新的、更有效的方法解决这些挑战。

Method: 该方法在拖车层面施加横向控制律，并在短视野范围内预测性地调整牵引车转向角，从而保证车辆在前进及倒车时都能稳定收敛于目标路径。此算法基于几何原理，无需每辆车的单独标定或训练。

Result: 在高保真物理仿真环境中，所提出的控制器在准确性、稳定性和对扰动的鲁棒性方面均可媲美或优于传统及最先进的路径跟踪算法。

Conclusion: 本文方法能够提升带挂车的自动化系统在多种场景下的路径跟踪性能，具有更强的通用性和实用性，为相关行业自动驾驶车辆的推广应用提供了有效技术支持。

Abstract: Fully autonomous tractor--trailer systems are increasingly deployed in logistics, agriculture, and industrial environments, where precise and robust path-tracking capabilities are essential. However, the articulation between the tractor and the trailer introduces additional nonlinearities and significantly complicates lateral and longitudinal control, particularly during reversing maneuvers. This paper introduces a novel path-tracking algorithm specifically designed for articulated vehicles with a single trailer. The proposed method combines a lateral control law applied at the trailer level with a short-horizon predictive adjustment of the tractor steering angle, ensuring stable convergence toward the desired path in both forward and backward motion. The approach is geometry-based and requires no per-vehicle calibration or training. Simulation studies in a high-fidelity physics simulator demonstrate the ability of the controller to match or outperform classical and state-of-the-art methods in terms of accuracy, stability, and robustness to disturbances.

</details>


### [211] [ZeroTouch: Tactile-Supervised Visual Contact Estimation for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21726)
*Dmitriy Kosenkov,Daniia Zinniatullina,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Iana Zhura,Mikhail Derevianchenko,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZeroTouch是一种利用触觉监督训练、但无需部署专用触觉传感器即可实现高精度抓取接触预测的框架，显著提升了抓取任务的成功率和接触力估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际机器人抓取任务中，准确估计物体与夹持器之间的物理交互极为重要，但依赖昂贵的触觉硬件会限制应用范围。本研究旨在利用视觉与本体信息，减少对专用触觉感测器的依赖，同时保证高精度的抓取预测。

Method: 提出ZeroTouch架构，利用触觉数据作为训练过程中的特权监督信息，模型在实际部署时仅需输入来自腕部的RGB图像、夹持器状态及重力方向即可预测密集接触变形、六轴力矩和适应不同抓取的压缩目标。

Result: 在完整的验证集上，ZeroTouch架构将法向力的平均绝对误差由基线模型的2.017N降至0.531N。在20次不同测试条件下，ZeroTouch在未见过的物体上成功率为95%，在已见物体但未见抓取条件下为80%，负载/内容变化下为90%，明显优于对比方法OpenVLA和SmolVLA。

Conclusion: ZeroTouch实现了无需部署期间专用触觉硬件的高性能抓取感知与控制，显著提升抓取成功率与估计精度，为机器人抓取任务的广泛应用提供了新的可能。

Abstract: Reliable robotic grasping benefits from estimating the evolving physical interaction and selecting a grasp-dependent compression target. Tactile sensors provide direct interaction measurements but require dedicated hardware at deployment. We introduce ZeroTouch, a tactile-supervised framework that predicts dense contact deformation, the instantaneous six-axis wrench, and a grasp-dependent desired compression target from wrist RGB observations, gripper state, and local gravity direction. Tactile measurements are used only as privileged supervision during training and are not required at deployment. On the full validation set, the complete architecture reduces normal-force MAE from 2.017 N for a state-only baseline to 0.531 N. In physical evaluation with 20 trials per condition, ZeroTouch achieves 95% success on an unseen object, 80% in a seen-object/unseen-grasp condition, and 90% under a content/load shift. Under the same evaluation protocol, OpenVLA achieves 25%, 40%, and 55%, while SmolVLA achieves 10%, 25%, and 35%, respectively.

</details>


### [212] [Visual Proactivity: Enhancing Human-Robot Collaboration Through Intent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21729)
*Valerio Bo,Edison Bejarano,Anaís Garrell,Alberto Sanfeliu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的视觉主动性方法，通过视觉反馈让机器人主动传达自身意图，从而改善人机协作中的透明度和交互流畅性。实验验证显示该方法提升了协作体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人与人类协作任务的增加，机器人不仅要理解人的意图，还需要能够主动表达自身意图，以提升合作效率和自然度。目前对机器人“传递自我意图”的研究相对较少。

Method: 作者提出“视觉主动性”策略，让机器人通过视觉反馈（如显示屏、灯光等形式）主动展示意图。并在“人向机器人递交物品”的场景下，设计并对比了反应式、预测式和主动式机器人行为。

Result: 用户研究表明，具备视觉主动性的机器人行为能够更好地促进人机一致性和协作协调性，提升人类用户对机器人意图的理解和配合程度。

Conclusion: 有效的视觉主动性可以提升人机协作的透明度和流畅性，为未来更智能、自然的人机协作铺平道路。

Abstract: As robots transition from performing repetitive tasks to collaborating with humans, understanding human intent becomes crucial to effective interaction. Anticipation enables robots to predict human actions, while proactivity allows them to take initiative and guide human behavior toward optimal outcomes. Although research has largely focused on how robots infer and respond to human intentions, less attention has been paid to how robots communicate their own intent. This paper introduces visual proactivity, a novel, simple yet effective approach that enables robots to communicate their intentions through visual feedback, influencing human behavior and enhancing transparency and fluency. We develop and evaluate proactive robotic behaviors in a human-to-robot handover scenario, where a user study validates human perception of reactive, anticipatory, and proactive behaviors. The results demonstrate that effective visual proactivity fosters better alignment and coordination, paving the way for more intuitive human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [213] [When Should Robots Intervene? Balancing Engagement and Intrusiveness in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21734)
*Lavinia Hriscu,Valerio Bo,Alberto Sanfeliu,Anaís Garrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了机器人在任务导向环境中设计交互策略时，如何在吸引用户参与和减少干扰性之间取得平衡，并通过模拟实验证明，适应性和情境敏感的干预更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人与机器人交互(HRI)在实际任务环境下常面临机器人打扰性强或互动不足的矛盾。为提升用户体验并确保机器人不会过分打扰用户，研究者亟需寻求合适的人机交互干预策略。

Method: 作者提出并比较了两种机器人干预策略：1）持续积极寻求互动策略；2）基于用户状态和任务情境的适应性选择性干预策略。两者均采用多模态行为信号（如身体朝向、注意力）驱动机器人行为。在模拟医院场景中，32名参与者被安排完成指定任务并接受这两种不同策略的机器人辅助。

Result: 实验发现，频繁的互动并不一定带来更好的用户参与。支持感和打扰感存在系统性权衡，这种平衡受物理距离和用户努力程度等因素影响。

Conclusion: 有效的HRI策略应依赖于情境感知和自适应的干预机制，而非单纯追求高频互动。研究为HRI设计人员提供了实证依据，强调智能互动策略的重要性。

Abstract: Designing effective Human-Robot Interaction in task-oriented settings requires carefully balancing user engagement with socially acceptable levels of robot intrusiveness. In this paper, we examine how different robot intervention strategies shape user experience, interaction dynamics, perceived intrusiveness, and sense of support. We compare two approaches: a continuous engagement-seeking robot strategy, and a context-aware strategy that selectively intervenes based on the user's state and task context. Both approaches rely on multimodal behavioral cues, including body orientation and attentional signals, to guide robot actions. We evaluate these strategies in a user study with 32 participants performing a task in a simulated hospital environment. Our findings show that higher interaction frequency does not necessarily lead to better engagement. Instead, we observe a systematic trade-off between perceived support and intrusiveness, influenced by factors such as physical proximity and user effort. These results provide empirical evidence that effective engagement in HRI depends on adaptive, context-sensitive intervention policies.

</details>


### [214] [Sandwich-Residuals: Parameter-Efficient Test-time Adaptation of World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21740)
*Krishnam Soni,Aditya Sehgal,Vedant Dave,Elmar Rueckert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新方法Sandwich-Residuals，用于在保持预训练世界模型参数不变的情况下，在线学习小的残差修正，实现高效的测试时自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的latent world模型在面对测试条件与训练条件不一致时，预测会失效。现有的测试时自适应方法通常需要大幅修改模型参数，操作复杂且计算量大。因此需要一种能高效、低成本实现模型自适应的新方案。

Method: Sandwich-Residuals方法在保持原有预训练模型冻结的情况下，只在预测器周围在线学习和添加小规模的残差修正。这些残差通过自监督方式根据模型预测误差在线优化，不需要任何奖励、手标注或源域数据。

Result: 在AdaJEPA基准的21种情况下，该方法在只需适应1%-3%参数的条件下，达到了1.3倍于冻结模型的成功率，且保留了最优AdaJEPA变体95%的性能。在更复杂的移位场景下，优势提升到1.9倍，并与复杂的内部结构调优方法相媲美。此外，方法也在3D操作任务DINO-WM模型上得到了验证。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，高效的世界模型测试时自适应并不一定需要修改预训练的内部权重，只需在预测器外围添加精巧的残差修正，即可实现在新环境下的有效适应。

Abstract: Latent world models enable planning by predicting the effects of actions in a learned representation space, but their predictions can become unreliable when test-time conditions differ from training. Existing test-time adaptation methods address this by updating parts of the pretrained model, often modifying millions of parameters and requiring a choice of which internal components to adapt. We introduce Sandwich-Residuals, a lightweight alternative that keeps the pretrained world model frozen and learns only small residual corrections around the predictor. The residuals are optimized online using the model's self-supervised prediction error and require no rewards, labels, or source-domain data. Across 21 conditions on the AdaJEPA benchmark, our method achieves $1.3\times$ the success rate of the frozen model while retaining 95% of the performance of the strongest AdaJEPA variant and adapting 97-99% fewer parameters. Under compound shifts, this advantage increases to $1.9\times$ the success rate of the frozen model, while remaining comparable to internal block adaptation. We further demonstrate the same adaptation principle on a DINO-WM model for 3-D manipulation. These results suggest that effective test-time adaptation of world models does not necessarily require modifying their pretrained internal weights.

</details>


### [215] [Understanding Engagement and Intrusiveness in Assistive Human-Robot Interaction Using Individual Traits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21744)
*Valerio Bo,Lavinia Hriscu,Alberto Sanfeliu,Anaís Garrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探讨机器人在协助用户完成不熟悉任务中的作用，发现个体特质对机器人辅助时的情感和互动体验有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于机器人距离对用户参与度影响的研究结论不一，有的认为更近距离提升互动，有的则认为会造成入侵感。研究动机在于厘清机器人在任务协助中的“入侵感”不仅受距离影响，干预频率及个体特质亦重要。

Method: 研究招募了32名参与者，安排他们与两台辅助机器人互动，这些机器人以不同的干预策略提供类似的任务协助。实验考查了用户的个体特质（如性格、人口统计）对体验的影响。

Result: 实验发现，整体参与度在不同条件下保持稳定，但用户的情感反应和对机器人入侵感的感知会随性格特质显著变化。不同的人格会根据机器人行为策略对互动动态产生不同影响。

Conclusion: 有效的人机交互应关注个体差异，调整机器人行为以保持用户参与度，同时尊重每位用户独有的情感和行为特征。

Abstract: Robot assistance is particularly crucial in unfamiliar tasks, where users must understand task requirements while coordinating with the robot. Previous research offers mixed evidence on the role of robot proxemics in user engagement: some studies suggest closer proximity enhances interaction, while others report it can feel intrusive. In this work, we argue that perceptions of intrusiveness depend not only on proxemics but also on the frequency of robot interventions, and are strongly influenced by individual traits such as personality and demographics. We conducted an experiment with 32 participants who interacted with two assistive robots that provided similar task support but differed in their intervention strategies. Results indicate that overall engagement remains stable across conditions, yet affective responses and perceived intrusiveness vary significantly with personality traits. Moreover, personality shapes interaction dynamics differently depending on the robot's behavior. These findings emphasize that effective human-robot interaction should account for individual differences, tailoring robot behavior to maintain engagement while respecting each user's unique affective and behavioral profile.

</details>


### [216] [ForceTwin: Physics-informed Digital Twins for Robotic Manipulation from Instrumented Human Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21751)
*Tim Engelbracht,René Zurbrügg,Mayank Mittal,Marco Hutter,Marc Pollefeys,Hermann Blum,Zuria Bauer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForceTwin是一种通过带力传感的夹持器交互，识别和建模可动物体状态相关动力学的系统，能生成更具物理一致性的数字孪生模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的方法主要恢复运动学或基于视觉和语言先验分配静态物理参数，无法识别和表达随配置与速度变化的机制动态属性，导致生成数字孪生模型不准确且局限。

Method: ForceTwin系统让人类使用带力传感的夹持器与物体交互，通过同步记录的位姿和交互力，估计多种参数，包括关节运动学、惯性、库仑摩擦、粘性阻尼，以及使用结构化神经网络捕捉状态依赖的机制动力。

Result: ForceTwin将惯性参数误差减半；在Spot和Franka FR3机器人上实现87%的任务完成率，优于仅基于先验（60%）或运动学（57%）生成的孪生体，对高机制属性物体提升最显著。识别得到的孪生体还能用于训练和实际部署全身动作策略。

Conclusion: ForceTwin大幅提升了可动物体物理属性识别和数字孪生模型的准确性，有助于更高效、真实地操控和交互复杂环境中的物体。

Abstract: Manipulating objects requires understanding not only their motion, but also the physical properties that determine it. For articulated objects, these include inertia, friction, and mechanisms such as springs or door closers, whose effects can vary with configuration and velocity. Such properties are not directly observable from appearance: visually identical doors may require very different effort to manipulate. Existing digital-twin pipelines recover primarily kinematics or assign static physical parameters from visual and language priors, which can yield physically implausible estimates. As a result, state-dependent mechanism dynamics remain unidentified and are not represented in standard asset formats. We present ForceTwin, a system for identifying physics-informed digital twins of articulated objects from instrumented human interaction. A person probes an object using a handheld force-sensing gripper, providing synchronized poses and interaction forces from which we estimate the articulation, parametric dynamics including inertia, Coulomb friction, viscous damping, and a structured neural residual capturing state-dependent mechanism forces. ForceTwin nearly halves the inertial-parameter error of a VLM prior. As a feedforward dynamics model for impedance control on a Spot and a Franka FR3, ForceTwin achieves 87% goal completion across nine object-embodiment pairs, compared with 60% using VLM-prior and 57% using kinematics-only twins, with the largest gains on objects whose strong mechanisms cause both baselines to stall. We further use the identified twins to train whole-body door-traversal policies and deploy them in the real world. Project Page: https://timengelbracht.github.io/forcetwin-website/

</details>


### [217] [PSR: Predictive Sensorimotor Representation Learning for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21753)
*Shengbao Li,Peng Xu,Chao Tang,Hao Wei,Jiaheng Wang,Hong Yin,Jiangtao Chen,Jinxuan Zhu,Zhong Zhou,Mengfan Wang,Tingguang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的用于力觉丰富操作任务的感知-运动预测表示（PSR）学习框架，能显著提升机器人高精度操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅被动利用力反馈，缺乏对未来接触动力学的主动预测，导致操作精度有限。本文旨在解决这个问题，提高复杂交互任务中的高精度控制能力。
